## Patent Rules {#patent-rules .Title-of-Act}

[SOR]{.abbr title="Statutory Orders and Regulations"}/2019-251
:::

[PATENT ACT](/eng/acts/P-4)

Registration 2019-06-25

Patent Rules

P.C. 2019-917 2019-06-22
::::

::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: section
::: {.section .order}
Her Excellency the Governor General in Council, on the recommendation of
the Minister of Industry, pursuant to section 12[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^a^](#fn_81000-2-2651_hq_24053){#fn_81000-2-2651_hq_24053-ID0EIDAA}
and subsection 20(18) of the [Patent Act](/eng/acts/P-4)[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^b^](#fn_81000-2-2651_hq_24054){#fn_81000-2-2651_hq_24054-ID0EFDAA}
and section 12[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^c^](#fn_81000-2-2651_hq_24055){#fn_81000-2-2651_hq_24055-ID0EDDAA}
of that Act as it read immediately before October 1, 1989, makes the
annexed [Patent Rules](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-423).

## [Interpretation]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1182096 .Part}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2021-131, s. 1(F)

\]
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  following definitions apply in these Rules.

  [[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the [Patent
      Act](/eng/acts/P-4). ([Loi]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Administrative Instructions]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Administrative Instructions]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/2022-120, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

  [[Administrative Instructions under the PCT]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Administrative Instructions under the
      PCT]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Administrative Instructions
      under the Patent Cooperation Treaty, including any modifications
      made from time to time. ([Instructions administratives du
      PCT]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[associate patent agent]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[associate patent agent]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a patent
      agent appointed by another patent agent under section
      28. ([coagent]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Budapest Treaty]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Budapest Treaty]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Budapest Treaty
      on the International Recognition of the Deposit of Microorganisms
      for the Purposes of Patent Procedure, done at Budapest on April
      28, 1977, including any amendments and revisions made from time to
      time to which Canada is a party. ([Traité de
      Budapest]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[common representative]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[common representative]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an applicant
      or patentee appointed under section 26, 218, 219 or
      220. ([représentant commun]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[description]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[description]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, except in Form 1 of
      Schedule 1, the part of a specification other than the
      claims. ([description]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[foreign practitioner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[foreign practitioner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an individual
      whose name is included in the Register of Patent Agents under
      section 19 of the [College of Patent Agents and Trademark Agents
      Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2021-129). ([professionnel
      étranger]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[international application]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[international application]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an
      application for a patent filed under the Patent Cooperation
      Treaty. ([demande internationale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[international depositary authority]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[international depositary authority]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the
      same meaning as in Article 2(viii) of the Budapest
      Treaty. ([autorité de dépôt internationale]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[national phase entry date]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[national phase entry date]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the date
      determined under subsection 155(2) or section 210, as
      applicable. ([date d'entrée en phase nationale]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[patent agent]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[patent agent]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2021-131, s.
      2\]]{.Repealed}

  [[Patent Cooperation Treaty]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Patent Cooperation Treaty]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Patent
      Cooperation Treaty, done at Washington on June 19, 1970, including
      any amendments, modifications and revisions made from time to time
      to which Canada is a party. ([Traité de coopération en matière de
      brevets]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[PCT national phase application]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[PCT national phase application]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an
      international application in respect of which the applicant

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} has complied with the requirements of
        subsection 154(1) and, if applicable, subsection 154(2); or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} has, before October 30, 2019, complied with the
        requirements of subsection 58(1) and, if applicable, subsection
        58(2) of the [Patent Rules](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-423), as
        they read immediately before that date. ([demande PCT à la phase
        nationale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[PCT sequence listing standard]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[PCT sequence listing standard]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means
      Standard ST.26 of the World Intellectual Property Organization,
      Recommended Standard for the Presentation of Nucleotide and Amino
      Acid Sequence Listings using XML (eXtensible Markup Language), as
      amended from time to time. ([norme PCT de listages des
      séquences]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[presentation date]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[presentation date]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the date
      determined under subsection 103(2) or 202(2), as
      applicable. ([date de soumission]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Regulations under the Budapest Treaty]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Regulations under the Budapest
      Treaty]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Regulations under the
      Budapest Treaty on the International Recognition of the Deposit of
      Microorganisms for the Purposes of Patent Procedure, including any
      amendments made from time to time. ([Règlement d'exécution du
      Traité de Budapest]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Regulations under the PCT]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Regulations under the PCT]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the
      Regulations under the Patent Cooperation Treaty, including any
      amendments made from time to time. ([Règlement d'exécution du
      PCT]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[sequence listing]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[sequence listing]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as
      in the PCT sequence listing standard. ([listage des
      séquences]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of
  [[drawing]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of the Act and these Rules,
  [[drawing]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes a photograph.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reference to period

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A reference to a period of time in these Rules is, if
  the period is extended under section 3 or subsection 160(2) of these
  Rules or subsection 78(1) of the Act, to be read as a reference to the
  period as extended.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Clarification

**[[2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2 .sectionLabel}** For greater certainty, for
the purposes of these Rules,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} an application for the reissue of a patent is not
  considered to be an application for a patent; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a patent granted on the basis of a divisional
  application that results from the division of an original application
  is not a patent granted on the basis of the original application.

## [PART 1]{.HLabel1}[Rules of General Application]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1182127 .Part}

### [Extension of Time]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1182128 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Time fixed by Rules

- **[[3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject
  to these Rules, the Commissioner is authorized to extend any period of
  time fixed by these Rules for doing anything --- whether that period
  has expired or not --- if the Commissioner considers that the
  circumstances justify the extension and if, before the end of that
  period, the extension is applied for and, except in the case of the
  period of time fixed by subsection 86(9), the fee set out in item 1 of
  Schedule 2 is paid.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Clarification

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, for the purpose of subsection
  (1), a period of time fixed by the Act that is dependent on a date
  that is prescribed by these Rules is not considered to be a period of
  time fixed by these Rules.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other authorized extensions

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Commissioner is authorized to extend the period
  of time for the payment of a fee referred to in subsection 44(1),
  68(1) or (2), 80(1), 85.1(5), 86(1), (1.1), (6), (10) or (12) or
  112(1), paragraph 112(5)(a) or (c) or 154(1)(c), subsection 154(2) or
  subparagraph 154(3)(a)(iii) or (b)(i) or (ii) after the expiry of that
  period if the Commissioner considers that the circumstances justify
  the extension and if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the amount of the small entity fee was paid before
    the end of that period;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it is later determined that the standard fee should
    have been paid;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the applicant or patentee files a statement that,
    to the best of their knowledge, the small entity fee was paid in
    good faith and the application for the extension is being filed
    without undue delay after the applicant or patentee became aware
    that the standard fee should have been paid;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the applicant or patentee pays the difference
    between the amount of the small entity fee that was paid and the
    standard fee that was applicable on the day on which the small
    entity fee was paid; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the applicant or patentee pays the fee set out in
    item 1 of Schedule 2.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension --- request for examination

  [(3.1)]{.lawlabel} The Commissioner is authorized to extend the period
  of time for the payment of a fee referred to in subsection 80(1) after
  the end of that period if the Commissioner considers that the
  circumstances justify the extension and

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the applicant paid an insufficient amount in a case
    other than one of the cases that meets the conditions set out in
    paragraphs (3)(a) and (b) or (4)(a) and (b) before the end of that
    period;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Commissioner sent a notice to the applicant
    acknowledging that the request for examination was made in
    accordance with subsection 35(2) of the Act;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} it was determined after the date of the notice that
    the payment was insufficient;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the applicant files a statement declaring that the
    application for the extension is being filed without undue delay
    after the applicant became aware that the payment was insufficient
    and declaring that the insufficient amount was paid unintentionally;
    and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the applicant pays the difference between the
    amount that was paid and the amount of the fee that was payable on
    the day on which the insufficient payment was made.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension --- erroneous information

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Commissioner is authorized to extend any period
  of time for the payment of a fee set out in Schedule 2 or 3 after the
  expiry of that period if the Commissioner considers that the
  circumstances justify the extension and if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the Commissioner provided erroneous information in
    writing concerning the amount of the fee;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the applicant or patentee paid an insufficient
    amount as a result of that erroneous information;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the applicant or patentee identifies the source of
    the erroneous information that was relied on and files a statement
    that the application for the extension is being filed without undue
    delay after the applicant or patentee became aware that the amount
    paid was insufficient, unless the Commissioner considers that the
    circumstances do not justify requiring the statement and the
    application; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the applicant or patentee pays the difference
    between the amount that was paid and the amount of the fee that was
    payable on the day on which the insufficient payment was made,
    unless the Commissioner waives payment of the difference under
    section 139.1.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension --- date of payment

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If under any of subsections (3) to (4), the
  Commissioner extends the period of time for the payment of a fee, that
  fee is deemed to have been paid on the day on which the small entity
  fee was paid or the insufficient payment was made, as the case may be.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application to subsection 128(1)

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (5) does not apply to the determination of
  the date of the payment of a fee for the purposes of subsection
  128(1).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Time fixed by subsection 18(2) of Act

**[[4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4 .sectionLabel}** The Commissioner is
authorized to extend the period of time fixed by subsection 18(2) of the
Act --- whether that period has expired or not --- if the Commissioner
considers that the circumstances justify the extension and if, before
the end of that period, the extension is applied for and the fee set out
in item 1 of Schedule 2 is paid.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prescribed days

**[[5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5 .sectionLabel}** The following days are
prescribed for the purposes of subsection 78(1) of the Act:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} Saturday;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} Sunday;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} January 1 or, if January 1 falls on a Saturday or a
  Sunday, the following Monday;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} Good Friday;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} Easter Monday;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} the Monday before May 25;

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} June 24 or, if June 24 falls on a Saturday or a
  Sunday, the following Monday;

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} July 1 or, if July 1 falls on a Saturday or a Sunday,
  the following Monday;

- [(i)]{.lawlabel} the first Monday in August;

- [(j)]{.lawlabel} the first Monday in September;

- [(j.1)]{.lawlabel} September 30 or, if September 30 falls on a
  Saturday or a Sunday, the following Monday;

- [(k)]{.lawlabel} the second Monday in October;

- [(l)]{.lawlabel} November 11 or, if November 11 falls on a Saturday or
  a Sunday, the following Monday;

- [(m)]{.lawlabel} December 25 and 26 or

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if December 25 falls on a Friday, that Friday and
    the following Monday, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if December 25 falls on a Saturday or a Sunday,
    the following Monday and Tuesday; and

- [(n)]{.lawlabel} any day on which the Patent Office is closed to the
  public for all or part of the day during ordinary business hours.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-44, s. 2](#1340200-1340710){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1340200-1340710.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-5-20191030.html "Link to previous version of section 5"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Prescribed Fee]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1374900 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fee deemed paid

**[[5.01]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5.01 .sectionLabel}** If these Rules set
out a small entity fee and a standard fee in respect of a patent or an
application for a patent, a standard fee is deemed to be paid on the day
on which a small entity fee is paid if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} on or before the day on which the small entity fee is
  paid, a small entity declaration is filed in respect of the
  application in accordance with subsection 44(3), or a small entity
  declaration is filed in accordance with subsection 112(3) in respect
  of the patent or in accordance with subsection 44(3) in respect of the
  application on the basis of which the patent was granted;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} it is later determined that the standard fee should
  have been paid;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} before the end of the period of time for the payment
  of the standard fee, the applicant or patentee pays the difference
  between the amount of the applicable small entity fee and the amount
  of the standard fee that was applicable on the day on which the small
  entity fee was paid; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the applicant or patentee files a statement declaring
  that the small entity fee was, to the best of their knowledge, paid in
  good faith and the difference between the amount of the applicable
  small entity fee and the amount of the standard fee that was
  applicable on the day on which the small entity fee was paid is being
  paid without undue delay after the applicant or patentee became aware
  that the standard fee should have been paid.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-241, s. 2](#1496899-1505558){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1496899-1505558.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amount due --- day of last partial
payment

- **[[5.02]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5.02
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 5.2, if a fee is
  paid by means of two or more partial payments, the amount of the fee
  to be paid is the amount that would be payable if the full amount of
  the fee had been paid on the day on which the last partial payment is
  received.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amount due --- day requirements met

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), the amount of the fee to
  be paid for an examination of an application for a patent under
  subsection 35(1) of the Act is the amount of the fee that is payable
  on the day on which the request for examination is made and the fee is
  paid or, if the request is made and the fee is paid on different days,
  the later of those days.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amount due --- day of amendment

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The amount of the fee to be paid for an examination
  of an application for a patent under subsection 35(1) of the Act is,
  if the applicant submits an amendment referred to in section 83.1, the
  amount of the fee that is payable on the day on which the applicant
  makes the amendment referred to in section 83.1.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-241, s. 2](#1496899-1505567){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1496899-1505567.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Small entity declaration filed after
payment

**[[5.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5.1 .sectionLabel}** Despite any provision
of these Rules that sets out a small entity fee and a standard fee in
respect of a patent or an application for a patent, if a patentee or
applicant pays the standard fee and a small entity declaration is later
filed in respect of that patent or application, the applicable fee is
the standard fee.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 3](#1353715-1374904){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353715-1374904.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension of time for payment of fee

**[[5.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5.2 .sectionLabel}** For greater certainty,
if the Commissioner extends the period of time for the payment of a fee
under subsection 3(3), (3.1) or (4), the applicable fee is

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an extension under subsection 3(3),
  the standard fee that was payable on the day on which the small entity
  fee was paid; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an extension under subsection 3(3.1)
  or (4), the fee that was payable on the day on which the insufficient
  payment was made.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 3](#1353715-1374910){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353715-1374910.html"
  role="button"}

  role="button"}

### [Communications]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1182161 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Written communications to Commissioner

**[[6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6 .sectionLabel}** Written communications
intended for the Commissioner or the Patent Office must be addressed to
the "Commissioner of Patents".

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Postal address

- **[[7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A person
  who is doing business before the Patent Office must provide the
  Commissioner with their postal address and a written communication
  sent by the Commissioner or the Patent Office to that person at that
  address is, unless the communication is withdrawn, considered to have
  been sent to that person on the date that it bears.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Electronic communications

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a person who is doing business before the Patent
  Office authorizes the sending of communications by electronic means
  specified by the Commissioner, a written communication sent by the
  Commissioner or the Patent Office to that person by those means is,
  unless the communication is withdrawn, considered to have been sent to
  that person on the date borne by the communication.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-241, s. 4](#1496906-1505574){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1496906-1505574.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-7-20191030.html "Link to previous version of section 7"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}One patent or application for patent per
communication

- **[[8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  written communication intended for the Commissioner or the Patent
  Office must not relate to more than one patent or one application for
  a patent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exceptions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in respect of a written
  communication relating to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a change in a name or an address;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a transfer;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a request to register a document;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a fee to maintain in effect an application for a
    patent or the rights accorded by a patent;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} an appointment, or revocation of an appointment, of
    a patent agent; or

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a correction of an error, if the error and
    correction are the same in each patent or application for a patent.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minimum content of written communications
--- applications

- **[[9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  written communication in respect of an application for a patent that
  is intended for the Commissioner or the Patent Office must include the
  name of the applicant and the application number or, if the
  application number is not known, information that allows the
  application to be identified.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Minimum content of written
  communications --- patents

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A written communication in respect of a patent that
  is intended for the Commissioner or the Patent Office must include the
  name of the patentee and the patent number.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Manner of submitting documents,
information or fees

- **[[10]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Unless
  they are submitted by electronic means under subsection 8.1(1) of the
  Act, any documents, information or fees must be submitted to the
  Commissioner or the Patent Office by physical delivery to the Patent
  Office or to an establishment that is designated by the Commissioner
  as being accepted for that purpose.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Date of receipt --- physical delivery
  to Patent Office

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Documents, information or fees that are submitted to
  the Commissioner or the Patent Office by physical delivery to the
  Patent Office are deemed to have been received by the Commissioner

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if they are delivered when the Office is open to
    the public, on the day on which they are delivered; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if they are delivered when the Office is closed to
    the public, on the first day on which the Office is next open to the
    public.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Date of receipt --- physical delivery
  to designated establishment

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Documents, information or fees that are submitted to
  the Commissioner or the Patent Office by physical delivery to a
  designated establishment are deemed to have been received by the
  Commissioner

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if they are delivered when the establishment is
    open to the public,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case where the Patent Office is open to
      the public for all or part of the day on which they are delivered,
      on that day, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, on the first day on which the
      Patent Office is next open to the public; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if they are delivered when the establishment is
    closed to the public, on the first day on which the Patent Office is
    next open to the public that falls on or after the day on which the
    establishment is next open to the public.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Date of receipt --- submission by
  electronic means

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Documents, information or fees that are submitted by
  electronic means under subsection 8.1(1) of the Act are deemed to have
  been received by the Commissioner on the day on which, according to
  the local time of the place where the Patent Office is located, the
  Patent Office receives them.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Date of receipt --- service provider

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (4), if documents, information or
  fees referred to in that subsection are first transmitted to a service
  provider authorized by the Commissioner to receive them and then are
  either transmitted or made available to the Commissioner or the Patent
  Office, they are deemed to have been received by the Commissioner or
  the Patent Office on the day on which, according to the local time of
  the place where the Patent Office is located, the service provider
  receives them.

:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Communication sent --- suspension,
revocation or surrender

**[[11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11 .sectionLabel}** Despite sections 34 and
35, if a patent agent's licence is suspended, revoked or surrendered,
any communication respecting a patent or an application for a patent
that is sent by the Commissioner or the Patent Office to that patent
agent is considered not to have been sent to the patentee or applicant
if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} it is sent within four months before the date of the
  suspension, revocation or surrender and no reply is provided before
  that date; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} it is sent on the date of the suspension, revocation
  or surrender.

::: HistoricalNote

  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1299102-1301287.html"
  role="button"}
:::

Version](section-11-20191030.html "Link to previous version of section 11"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Acknowledgment by Commissioner

**[[12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12 .sectionLabel}** A written communication
submitted to the Commissioner in respect of a filing under section 34.1
of the Act and a written communication submitted to the Commissioner
before the granting of a patent with the stated or apparent intention of
protesting against the granting of that patent must be acknowledged, but
information must not be given as to the action taken unless the
application for the patent is open to public inspection at the Patent
Office.

### [Presentation of Documents to Commissioner or Patent Office]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1182215 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Manner of submission

- **[[13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), documents
  submitted in paper form in connection with a patent and an application
  for a patent must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} be submitted on sheets of white paper that are free
    of creases and folds and that are 21.6 cm x 27.9 cm (8.5" x 11") or
    21 cm x 29.7 cm (A4 format);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} be submitted in a manner that permits direct
    reproduction by the Patent Office; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} be free of interlineations, cancellations or
    corrections.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Certified copies of documents and documents
  concerning transfers referred to in section 49 of the Act may be
  submitted on sheets of paper that are not larger than 21.6 cm x
  35.6 cm (8.5" x 14").

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Layout

- **[[14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-14
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), the
  contents of each page of a document must be in an upright position.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If it aids in presentation, figures, tables and
  chemical or mathematical formulas may appear sideways.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 4](#1353717-1374914){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353717-1374914.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-14-20191030.html "Link to previous version of section 14"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Documents in English or French

- **[[15]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-15 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} All
  documents or information submitted or made available to the
  Commissioner or the Patent Office must be in English or French, except
  for

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a document submitted or made available under
    paragraph 67(2)(b) or 72(3)(a) or subsection 74(1);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the specification and the drawings included in an
    application for a patent on its filing date that are contained in a
    document referred to in paragraph 71(d);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a document submitted under paragraph 85(1)(b);

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a copy of an international application submitted
    under paragraph 154(1)(a);

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} text matter contained in a sequence listing; or

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a document or information that is deemed to have
    been received by the Commissioner under section 156.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Translation --- previously filed
  application for patent

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a copy of a previously filed application for a
  patent is submitted or made available under paragraph 67(2)(b) and all
  or part of that application is in a language other than English or
  French, the applicant must submit to the Commissioner an English or
  French translation of the application or part of the application in
  question.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Translation --- specification and
  drawings

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If all or part of the text matter of the
  specification or the drawings that are contained in a document
  referred to in paragraph 71(d) that is submitted for the purposes of
  subsection 28(1) of the Act are in a language other than English or
  French, the applicant must submit to the Commissioner an English or
  French translation of that text matter.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sequence listing --- English and French

  [(3.1)]{.lawlabel} If a sequence listing contains text matter that is
  both in English and in French, only the following version of the text
  matter is taken into account for the purpose of interpreting the scope
  of protection sought or obtained:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the sequence listing contains an indication that
    either English or French is the original language of that text
    matter, the version that is in that original language; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the version that is in the same
    language as the claims.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sequence listing --- other language

  [(3.2)]{.lawlabel} Language-dependent free text that is contained in a
  sequence listing and that is in a language other than English or
  French is not taken into account for the purpose of interpreting the
  scope of protection sought or obtained.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice requiring translation

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If an applicant does not submit a translation
  required by subsection (2) or (3), the Commissioner must by notice
  require the applicant to submit the translation to the Commissioner
  not later than two months after the date of the notice.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Translation replaces original

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A translation submitted under subsection (2) or (3),
  or submitted after the notice referred to in subsection (4) is sent,
  replaces the text matter that was in a language other than English or
  French.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} A translation of all or part of the text matter in a
  specification or a drawing, submitted under subsection (2) or (3), or
  submitted after the notice referred to in subsection (4) is sent, must
  not contain any matter not reasonably to be inferred from the
  specification or the drawings contained in the application on its
  filing date.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of subsection 3(1)

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} Subsection 3(1) does not apply in respect of the time
  referred to in subsection (4).

### [Confidentiality]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1182249 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information respecting application for
patent

**[[16]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-16 .sectionLabel}** Unless otherwise required
by law, the Commissioner and the Patent Office must not provide any
information in respect of an application for a patent that is not open
to public inspection at the Patent Office to any person other than

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the applicant or, if there are joint applicants, any
  of the applicants;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a patent agent appointed in respect of that
  application; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} a person authorized by

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the applicant, if there is a single applicant,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the common representative, if there are joint
    applicants, or

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a patent agent appointed in respect of that
    application.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2021-131, s. 5](#1299106-1301295){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1299106-1301295.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-16-20191030.html "Link to previous version of section 16"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prescribed date --- withdrawal of request
for priority

**[[17]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-17 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
subsection 10(4) of the Act, if a request for priority is withdrawn with
respect to a previously regularly filed application for a patent, the
prescribed date is the earlier of

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the day on which a period of 16 months after the
  filing date of the previously regularly filed application expires, and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the request for priority is based on more than one
  previously regularly filed application, the day on which a period of
  16 months after the earliest of the filing dates of those applications
  expires.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prescribed date --- withdrawn application

**[[18]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-18 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
subsection 10(5) of the Act, the prescribed date is the earlier of

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the day that is two months before the expiry date of
  the confidentiality period referred to in subsection 10(2) of the Act,
  and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, the day on which the applicant submits
  their approval under subsection 10(2) of the Act for the application
  for a patent to be open to public inspection before the expiry of the
  confidentiality period.

### [Appointment of Common Representative]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1182315 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Power of joint applicants to appoint
common representative

- **[[26]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-26 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If, in
  respect of an application for a patent, there are joint applicants,
  one applicant may be appointed by the other applicants as their common
  representative.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Power of joint patentees to appoint
  common representative

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If, in respect of a patent, there are joint
  patentees, one patentee may be appointed by the other patentees as
  their common representative.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Manner of appointment

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The appointment of a common representative must be
  made by one of the following methods:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a patent or an application for a
    patent, in a notice to that effect that is signed by the other
    applicants or patentees and submitted to the Commissioner;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of an application for a patent --- other
    than a divisional application or an international application ---
    that includes a petition on the filing date, in that petition; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a PCT national phase application, in
    a notice to that effect that is submitted to the Commissioner on or
    before the national phase entry date of that application.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Common representative by default ---
  application for patent

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (6), (9) and (11), in respect
  of an application for a patent --- other than a divisional application
  --- in relation to which there are joint applicants and no common
  representative is appointed under subsection (3), the following person
  is deemed to be appointed as the common representative:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of an application for a patent, other
    than a PCT national phase application,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case where the application included a
      petition on the filing date, the first person named as an
      applicant in that petition,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case where, on the filing date, the
      application did not include a petition but did include a single
      other document naming the joint applicants, the joint applicant
      whose name appears first in that document, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the joint applicant whose
      name appears first when listed in alphabetical order on the filing
      date; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a PCT national phase application,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if joint applicants are named in the
      corresponding request under Article 4 of the Patent Cooperation
      Treaty, if more than one applicant complied with the requirements
      of subsection 154(1) and, if applicable, subsection 154(2), and if
      the first person named as an applicant in that request is one of
      those applicants, the first person named as an applicant in that
      request, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the joint applicant who
      complied with the requirements of subsection 154(1) and, if
      applicable, subsection 154(2), and whose name appears first when
      listed in alphabetical order.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Common representative by default ---
  divisional application

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (6), (9) and (11), in respect
  of a divisional application, in relation to which there are joint
  applicants and no common representative is appointed under paragraph
  (3)(a), the following person is deemed to be appointed as the common
  representative:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if, at the end of the day on the presentation date
    of the divisional application, the person who was the common
    representative in respect of the original application was an
    applicant of the divisional application, that person; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the first person named as an
    applicant in the petition contained in the divisional application on
    its presentation date.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Common representative by default ---
  correction or decision

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (9) and (11), in respect of an
  application for a patent in relation to which there are joint
  applicants and no common representative is appointed under subsection
  (3), if a correction has been made with respect to the naming of
  applicants under section 104 or subsection 154(6) and the correction
  has changed the identity of the applicants, or if a decision is made
  by the Commissioner under subsection 31(2), (3) or (4) of the Act,
  other than a decision refusing an application under one of those
  subsections, the joint applicant whose name appears first when listed
  in alphabetical order after that correction or decision --- or if
  there is more than one correction or decision, or both a correction
  and a decision, after the most recent of those corrections or
  decisions --- is deemed to be appointed as the common representative.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Common representative by default ---
  patent

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (9) and (11), in respect of a
  patent --- other than a reissued patent --- in relation to which there
  are joint patentees and no common representative is appointed under
  paragraph (3)(a), the person who, immediately before the patent was
  granted, was the common representative in respect of the application
  on the basis of which the patent was granted, is deemed to be
  appointed as the common representative in respect of the patent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Common representative by default ---
  reissued patent

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (9) and (11), in respect of a
  reissued patent, in relation to which there are joint patentees and no
  common representative is appointed under paragraph (3)(a), the person
  who was the common representative in respect of the original patent
  immediately before the patent was reissued is deemed to be appointed
  as the common representative in respect of the reissued patent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Common representative by default ---
  transfer of rights of single applicant or patentee

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (11), in the case where, under
  section 49 of the Act, the Commissioner, on or after the applicable
  date referred to in subsection (10), recorded the transfer of all or
  part of the rights of an applicant in an application for a patent or
  all or part of the rights of a patentee in a patent, as set out in the
  records of the Patent Office immediately before the transfer is
  recorded, that applicant was the only applicant of the application
  immediately before the transfer was recorded, or that patentee was the
  only patentee of the patent immediately before the transfer was
  recorded, no other person has been a single applicant of that
  application or a single patentee of that patent since that transfer
  was recorded and no common representative has been appointed under
  subsection (3) in respect of that application or that patent, the
  following person is deemed to be appointed as the common
  representative in respect of that patent or application:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if, immediately after the transfer is recorded, the
    person who transferred those rights is still an applicant of the
    application or a patentee of the patent, that person; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if, immediately after the transfer is recorded, the
    person who transferred those rights is no longer an applicant of the
    application or a patentee of the patent, the transferee whose name
    appears first in the request to record the transfer.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Applicable date

  [(10)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (9), the applicable
  date is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of an application for a patent, other
    than a PCT national phase application or a divisional application,
    the filing date of the application;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a PCT national phase application, the
    national phase entry date;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a divisional application, the
    presentation date of the application;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} despite paragraphs (a) to (c), in respect of an
    application in relation to which one or more corrections or
    decisions referred to in subsection (6) have been made, the date of
    that correction or decision or, if there is more than one correction
    or decision, or both a correction and a decision, the date of the
    most recent of those corrections or decisions;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a patent, other than a reissued
    patent, the date on which the patent was granted; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a reissued patent, the date on which
    the patent was reissued.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Common representative by default ---
  transfer of rights of common representative

  [(11)]{.lawlabel} If, under section 49 of the Act, the Commissioner
  records the transfer of all of the rights of a common representative
  in a patent or an application for a patent, as set out in the records
  of the Patent Office immediately before the transfer is recorded, and,
  immediately after the transfer is recorded, there is still more than
  one applicant for the patent or more than one patentee for the patent
  but no other common representative is appointed under paragraph
  (3)(a), the following person is deemed to be appointed as the common
  representative in respect of that patent or application:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the rights of the common representative are
    transferred to a single person, that person; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the rights of the common representative are
    transferred to more than one person, the transferee whose name
    appears first in the request to record the transfer.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revocation of appointment

  [(12)]{.lawlabel} An appointment of a common representative, including
  a deemed appointment, is revoked by the subsequent appointment of
  another common representative under paragraph (3)(a) or subsection
  (11).

  role="button"}

### [Appointment of Patent Agents]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1182363 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Power to appoint patent agent

- **[[27]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-27 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  applicant for a patent, a patentee or other person may appoint either
  one patent agent or all the patent agents at the same firm to
  represent them before the Patent Office in respect of a patent or an
  application for a patent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Mandatory appointment of patent agent

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An applicant for a patent must appoint one patent
  agent or all the patent agents at the same firm to represent them
  before the Patent Office in respect of their application for a patent
  if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the application is filed by a person other than the
    inventor;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} there is more than one inventor and the application
    is not filed jointly by all of the inventors; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a transfer, in whole or in part, of the application
    has been recorded by the Commissioner under section 49 of the Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Manner of appointment

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The appointment of one patent agent or all the patent
  agents at the same firm by an applicant for a patent or a patentee
  must be made by one of the following methods:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a patent or an application for a
    patent, in a notice to that effect that is submitted to the
    Commissioner and signed by

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if there is a single applicant or patentee, the
      applicant or patentee,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if there is a single applicant or patentee and a
      document authorizing a foreign practitioner to appoint a patent
      agent is signed by the applicant or patentee and submitted at the
      same time to the Commissioner, that foreign practitioner,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} if there are joint applicants or patentees, the
      common representative, or

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} if there are joint applicants or patentees and a
      document authorizing a foreign practitioner to appoint a patent
      agent is signed by the common representative and submitted at the
      same time to the Commissioner, that foreign practitioner;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of an application for a patent --- other
    than a divisional application or an international application ---
    that includes a petition on the filing date, in that petition;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a PCT national phase application, in
    a notice to that effect submitted to the Commissioner on or before
    the national phase entry date of that application; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a divisional application, in the
    petition included in the application on its presentation date.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Manner of appointment by another person

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The appointment, other than as an associate patent
  agent, of one patent agent or all the patent agents at the same firm,
  by a person other than an applicant for a patent or a patentee to
  represent them before the Patent Office in respect of a patent or an
  application for a patent must be made in a notice to that effect
  submitted to the Commissioner and signed by

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that person; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if a document signed by that person authorizing a
    foreign practitioner to appoint a patent agent is submitted at the
    same time to the Commissioner, that foreign practitioner.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consent to appointment

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If a person, other than a patent agent, submits to
  the Commissioner a document appointing, other than as an associate
  patent agent, one patent agent or all the patent agents at the same
  firm, the appointment is not effective until evidence of consent to
  that appointment by that one patent agent or by any of the patent
  agents at that firm is submitted to the Commissioner.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Patent agent by default --- patent

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The patent agent or all the patent agents at the same
  firm appointed in respect of an application for a patent under
  subsection 27(3) or (4) are deemed, unless the appointment document
  indicates otherwise, to have also been appointed in respect of any
  patent granted on the basis of the application.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revocation --- appointment by applicant
  or patentee

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} The appointment, including a deemed appointment, of
  one patent agent or all the patent agents at the same firm in respect
  of a patent or an application for a patent by an applicant for a
  patent or a patentee is revoked if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a notice to that effect is submitted to the
    Commissioner and signed by that one patent agent or any of the
    patent agents at that firm or by

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if there is a single applicant or patentee, the
      applicant or patentee,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if there is a single applicant or patentee and a
      document authorizing a foreign practitioner to revoke the
      appointment is signed by the applicant or patentee and is
      submitted at the same time to the Commissioner, that foreign
      practitioner,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} if there are joint applicants or patentees, the
      common representative, or

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} if there are joint applicants or patentees and a
      document signed by the common representative authorizing a foreign
      practitioner to revoke the appointment is submitted at the same
      time to the Commissioner, that foreign practitioner; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the licence of that one patent agent, or each of
    the patent agents at that firm, is suspended, revoked or
    surrendered.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revocation --- appointment by another
  person

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} The appointment, including a deemed appointment,
  other than as an associate patent agent, of one patent agent or all
  the patent agents at the same firm, in respect of a patent or an
  application for a patent by a person other than an applicant for a
  patent or a patentee is revoked if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a notice to that effect, signed by that person, by
    that one patent agent or by any of the patent agents at that firm,
    is submitted to the Commissioner;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a document signed by that person authorizing a
    foreign practitioner to revoke the appointment is submitted to the
    Commissioner at the same time as a notice revoking the appointment
    signed by that foreign practitioner; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the licence of that one patent agent, or each of
    the patent agents at that firm, is suspended, revoked or
    surrendered.

  role="button"}
:::

:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Power to appoint associate patent agent

- **[[28]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-28 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  patent agent appointed by an applicant for a patent, a patentee or
  other person to represent them before the Patent Office in respect of
  a patent or an application for a patent may appoint one patent agent
  or all the patent agents at the same firm as an associate patent agent
  in respect of that patent or application.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2021-131, s. 8\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Manner of appointment

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The appointment of one patent agent or all the patent
  agents at the same firm as an associate patent agent must be made by
  one of the following methods:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a patent or an application for a
    patent, in a notice to that effect that is signed by the patent
    agent appointing the associate patent agent and submitted to the
    Commissioner;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of an application for a patent --- other
    than a divisional application or an international application ---
    that includes a petition on the filing date, in that petition if the
    petition is submitted by a patent agent;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a PCT national phase application, in
    a notice to that effect submitted to the Commissioner by a patent
    agent on or before the national phase entry date of that
    application; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a divisional application, in the
    petition included in the application on its presentation date if the
    petition is submitted by a patent agent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Associate patent agent by default ---
  patents

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Any associate patent agent appointed in respect of an
  application for a patent under subsection (3) is deemed, unless the
  appointment document indicates otherwise, to have also been appointed
  in respect of any patent granted on the basis of the application.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revocation of appointment --- one
  associate patent agent acting alone

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The appointment, including a deemed appointment, of
  one patent agent as an associate patent agent in respect of a patent
  or an application for a patent is revoked if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case where the associate patent agent was
    appointed by one patent agent who was appointed under subsection
    27(3) or (4),

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a notice of revocation signed by the associate
      patent agent or the patent agent is submitted to the Commissioner,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the associate patent agent's licence is
      suspended, revoked or surrendered, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the appointment of the patent agent is revoked;
      and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case where the associate patent agent was
    appointed by one of the patent agents at the same firm who were
    appointed under subsection 27(3) or (4),

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a notice of revocation signed by the associate
      patent agent or by one of the patent agents at the firm is
      submitted to the Commissioner,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the associate patent agent's licence is
      suspended, revoked or surrendered, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the appointment of all the patent agents at the
      firm is revoked.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revocation of appointment --- associate
  patent agent

  [(5.1)]{.lawlabel} The appointment, including a deemed appointment, as
  an associate patent agent, of all the patent agents at the same firm
  in respect of a patent or an application for a patent is revoked if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case where they were appointed by one patent
    agent who was appointed under subsection 27(3) or (4),

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a notice of revocation signed by one of the
      patent agents at the firm acting as an associate patent agent or
      by the patent agent who appointed them is submitted to the
      Commissioner,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the licence of each of the patent agents acting
      as an associate patent agent is suspended, revoked or surrendered,
      or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the appointment of the patent agent is revoked;
      and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case where they were appointed by one of the
    patent agents at the same firm who were appointed under subsection
    27(3) or (4),

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a notice of revocation signed by one of the
      patent agents at the firm acting as an associate patent agent, or
      by one of the patent agents at the firm that appointed them, is
      submitted to the Commissioner,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the licence of each of the patent agents acting
      as an associate patent agent is suspended, revoked or surrendered,
      or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the appointment under subsection 27(3) or (4)
      of all the patent agents at the firm is revoked.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Patent agent --- member of firm

**[[28.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-28.1 .sectionLabel}** If all the patent
agents at the same firm have been appointed under section 27 or 28, the
following rules apply:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a patent agent who becomes a member of the firm after
  the appointment is deemed to be appointed beginning on the day on
  which they become a member of the firm;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a person who is a member of the firm who becomes a
  patent agent after the appointment is deemed to have been appointed
  beginning on the day on which they become a patent agent;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the appointment of a patent agent who ceases to be a
  member of the firm is deemed to be revoked on the day on which they
  cease to be a member of the firm; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the appointment of a patent agent whose licence is
  suspended, revoked or surrendered is deemed to be revoked on the day
  on which their licence is suspended, revoked or surrendered.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2021-131, s. 9](#1299128-1301373){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1299128-1301373.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appointment document --- one patent agent

- **[[29]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  document appointing one patent agent must include the name and postal
  address of that patent agent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appointment document --- all the patent
  agents

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A document appointing all the patent agents at the
  same firm must include the name and postal address of the firm. An
  appointment document that includes only the name and postal address of
  the firm is sufficient to appoint all the patent agents at that firm.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2021-131, s. 9](#1299128-1301380){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1299128-1301380.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-29-20191030.html "Link to previous version of section 29"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Written communications

**[[29.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29.1 .sectionLabel}** If all the patent
agents at the same firm have been appointed or are deemed to have been
appointed under section 27 or 28, any written communication sent by the
Commissioner or the Patent Office to the firm is considered to have been
sent to all of the patent agents at the firm.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2021-131, s. 9](#1299128-1301384){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1299128-1301384.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Patent agent by default --- transfer

**[[30]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-30 .sectionLabel}** The patent agent or all
the patent agents at the same firm who were appointed, other than as an
associate patent agent, to represent an applicant or patentee before the
Patent Office are, unless otherwise indicated in a request to record a
transfer under subsection 49(2) or (3) of the Act, deemed to have been
appointed by the transferee in respect of the patent or the application
that is the subject of the recording.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2021-131, s. 9](#1299128-1301388){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1299128-1301388.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-30-20191030.html "Link to previous version of section 30"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice requiring appointment of patent
agent

**[[31]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-31 .sectionLabel}** If an applicant for a
patent is required under subsection 27(2) to appoint a patent agent but
no patent agent is appointed, the Commissioner must by notice to the
applicant require that, not later than three months after the date of
the notice, the applicant appoint a patent agent.

  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1299128-1301392.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Previous
Version](section-31-20191030.html "Link to previous version of section 31"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Successor patent agent

- **[[32]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-32 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  patent agent who, in respect of a patent or application for a patent,
  demonstrates to the Commissioner that they are the successor to a
  patent agent or all the patent agents at the same firm who were
  appointed under section 27 or 28 and who have since withdrawn from
  practice is deemed to be appointed under section 27 or 28, as the case
  may be, in respect of that patent or application.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Successor patent agents

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} All the patent agents at the same firm who, in
  respect of a patent or application for a patent, demonstrate to the
  Commissioner that they are the successors to a patent agent or all the
  patent agents at the same firm who were appointed under section 27 or
  28 and who have since withdrawn from practice are deemed to be
  appointed under section 27 or 28, as the case may be, in respect of
  that patent or application.

::: HistoricalNote

  role="button"}

### [Representation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1182432 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of act by common representative

**[[33]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-33 .sectionLabel}** Subject to sections 36
and 37, in any business before the Patent Office, an act done in respect
of a patent or an application for a patent, by or in relation to a
common representative, has the effect of an act done by or in relation
to all of the applicants or patentees.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of act by patent agent

**[[34]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-34 .sectionLabel}** Subject to sections 36
and 37, in any business before the Patent Office, an act done in respect
of a patent or an application for a patent, by or in relation to a
patent agent, other than an associate patent agent, who is appointed in
respect of that application or patent, has the same effect as an act
done by or in relation to the applicant for a patent, a patentee or
other person who appointed the patent agent.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2021-131, s. 10](#1299130-1301396){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1299130-1301396.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-34-20191030.html "Link to previous version of section 34"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of act by associate patent agent

**[[35]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-35 .sectionLabel}** Subject to sections 36
and 37, in any business before the Patent Office, an act done in respect
of a patent or an application for a patent, by or in relation to an
associate patent agent appointed in respect of that application or
patent, has the same effect as an act done by or in relation to the
applicant for a patent, a patentee or other person who appointed the
patent agent who appointed the associate patent agent.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prosecuting or maintaining in effect
application for patent

- **[[36]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-36
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (2) to (5),
  only the following persons are authorized to act in any business
  before the Patent Office for the purpose of prosecuting or maintaining
  in effect an application for a patent:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if, in respect of the application, a patent agent
    is appointed or there is a requirement under subsection 27(2) to
    appoint a patent agent, the patent agent who is appointed; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if there is a single applicant, the applicant,
      and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if there are joint applicants, the common
      representative.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exceptions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of filing an application for a
  patent, paying a fee under subsection 27(2) or section 27.1 of the
  Act, paying the additional fee for late payment referred to in
  subsection 154(4) of these Rules or complying with the requirements of
  subsection 154(1), (2) or (3) of these Rules,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if there is a single applicant, they must represent
    themselves or be represented by a person authorized by the
    applicant; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if there are joint applicants, they must be
    represented by one of the applicants or by a person authorized by
    one of the applicants.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exceptions

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of submitting a request to record a
  transfer under subsection 49(2) of the Act,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if there is a single applicant, they must represent
    themselves or be represented by a person authorized by the
    applicant; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if there are joint applicants,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case where the transfer is a transfer of
      the right or interest of a single joint applicant, the joint
      applicants must be represented by that joint applicant or by the
      common representative, or by a person authorized by that joint
      applicant or by the common representative, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the joint applicants must be
      represented by the common representative or by a person authorized
      by the common representative.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exceptions

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of submitting a request to record a
  name change under section 125,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if there is a single applicant, they must represent
    themselves or be represented by a person authorized by the
    applicant; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if there are joint applicants, they must be
    represented by the common representative or by a person authorized
    by the common representative.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exceptions

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of section 27.01 or 28.01 of the
  Act, for the purpose of paying a fee in respect of an application for
  a patent --- other than a fee under subsection 27(2) or section 27.1
  of the Act or a fee referred to in subsection 154(1), (2), (3) or (4)
  of these Rules --- or for the purpose of taking any of the actions
  required by subparagraphs 73(3)(a)(i) to (iv) of the Act to reinstate
  an application for a patent deemed to be abandoned under paragraph
  73(1)(c) of the Act,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if there is a single applicant, they may represent
    themselves; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if there are joint applicants, they may be
    represented by the common representative.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2021-131, s. 11](#1299132-1301403){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1299132-1301403.html"
  role="button"}

  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353732-1374942.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-36-20210628.html "Link to previous version of section 36"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Procedure relating to patent

- **[[37]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-37
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), in any
  business before the Patent Office for the purpose of a procedure
  relating to a patent,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if there is a single patentee, they must represent
    themselves or be represented by any person authorized by them; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if there are joint patentees,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} for the purpose of paying a fee under section 46
      of the Act, the patentees must be represented by one of the
      patentees or by a person authorized by one of the patentees,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} for the purpose of submitting a request to
      record a transfer under subsection 49(3) of the Act,

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} in the case where the transfer is a transfer of
        the right or interest of a single joint patentee, the joint
        patentees must be represented by that joint patentee or by the
        common representative, or by a person authorized by that joint
        patentee or by the common representative, and

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the joint patentees must be
        represented by the common representative or a person authorized
        by the common representative, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} for any other purpose, the patentees must be
      represented by the common representative or by a person authorized
      by the common representative.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exceptions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In any business before the Patent Office for the
  purposes of making observations under subsection 117.11(7), granting
  an additional term under section 46.1 of the Act, reissuing a patent
  under section 47 of the Act, making a disclaimer under section 48 of
  the Act, filing a reply under subsection 48.2(5) of the Act or
  participating in a re-examination proceeding under section 48.3 of the
  Act,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if there is a single patentee, they must represent
    themselves or be represented by a patent agent who has been
    appointed in respect of that business; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if there are joint patentees, the patentees must be
    represented by the common representative or by a patent agent who
    has been appointed in respect of that business.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2021-131, s. 12](#1299134-1301407){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1299134-1301407.html"
  role="button"}

  role="button"}
