## [PART 2]{.HLabel1}[Patent Cooperation Treaty]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1183298 .Part}

### [Definitions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1183299 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[141]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-141 .sectionLabel}** The following
definitions apply in this Part.

[[international filing date]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[international filing date]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the date
    accorded to an international application under Article 11 of the
    Patent Cooperation Treaty. ([date du dépôt
    international]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[priority date]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[priority date]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
    Article 2(xi) of the Patent Cooperation Treaty. ([date de
    priorité]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

### [Application of Treaty]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1183304 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}International applications

- **[[142]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-142
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2) and
  subsections 154(11) and (12), the provisions of the Patent Cooperation
  Treaty, the Regulations under the PCT and the Administrative
  Instructions under the PCT apply in respect of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an international application filed with the
    Commissioner; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an international application in which Canada is
    designated under the Patent Cooperation Treaty.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exceptions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Article 24(2) of the Patent Cooperation Treaty and
  Rule 49.6 of the Regulations under the PCT do not apply in respect of
  an international application in which Canada is designated under the
  Patent Cooperation Treaty.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 54](#1353866-1375365){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353866-1375365.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-142-20191030.html "Link to previous version of section 142"){.PITLink}
:::

### [International Phase]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1183312 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Receiving Office

**[[143]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-143 .sectionLabel}** If an international
application is filed with the Commissioner and the applicant or, if
there is more than one applicant, at least one of the applicants, is a
national or resident of Canada, the Commissioner must act as a receiving
Office in accordance with the Patent Cooperation Treaty, the Regulations
under the PCT and the Administrative Instructions under the PCT.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 54](#1353866-1375369){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353866-1375369.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-143-20191030.html "Link to previous version of section 143"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application in English or French

**[[144]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-144 .sectionLabel}** An international
application, other than any text matter contained in a sequence listing,
that is filed with the Commissioner must be entirely in English or
entirely in French.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}International Searching Authority and
International Preliminary Examining Authority

**[[145]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-145 .sectionLabel}** The Commissioner must
act as an International Searching Authority and an International
Preliminary Examining Authority in accordance with the Patent
Cooperation Treaty, the Regulations under the PCT and the Administrative
Instructions under the PCT.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 54](#1353866-1375373){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353866-1375373.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-145-20191030.html "Link to previous version of section 145"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appointment of all the patent agents

**[[145.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-145.1 .sectionLabel}** If all of the
agents at the same firm are appointed under Rule 90 of the Regulations
under the PCT to represent an applicant before the Commissioner, section
28.1, subsection 29(2) and section 29.1 apply in respect of the
appointment.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2021-131, s. 22](#1299163-1301466){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1299163-1301466.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fees payable in Canadian currency

- **[[146]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-146 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Fees
  payable under Rules 15 and 57 of the Regulations under the PCT must be
  paid in Canadian currency.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Patent Cooperation Treaty Fund

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Money received under Rules 15 and 57 of the
  Regulations under the PCT must be deposited in the account entitled
  the Patent Cooperation Treaty Fund within the account entitled the
  Canadian Intellectual Property Office Revolving Fund and must be paid
  out of that account in accordance with those Rules.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transmittal fee

**[[147]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-147 .sectionLabel}** An applicant of an
international application filed with the Commissioner must pay the
transmittal fee set out in item 16 of Schedule 2 for the performance of
the tasks referred to in Rule 14 of the Regulations under the PCT.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Search fee

**[[148]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-148 .sectionLabel}** An applicant of an
international application filed with the Commissioner must pay the
search fee set out in item 17 of Schedule 2 for the performance of the
tasks referred to in Rule 16 of the Regulations under the PCT.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Late payment fee

**[[148.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-148.1 .sectionLabel}** An applicant of an
international application filed with the Commissioner must pay the late
payment fee set out in Rule 16*bis*.2 of the Regulations under the PCT
if an invitation is sent to that applicant by the Commissioner under
Rule 16*bis*.1(a) of the Regulations under the PCT.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2023-113, s. 5](#1401205-1437107){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1401205-1437107.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional fee

**[[149]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-149 .sectionLabel}** The amount of the
additional fee for searching under Article 17(3)(a) of the Patent
Cooperation Treaty is the fee set out in item 18 of Schedule 2.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Preliminary examination fee

**[[150]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-150 .sectionLabel}** An applicant of an
international application filed with the Commissioner who requests an
international preliminary examination must pay the preliminary
examination fee set out in item 19 of Schedule 2 for the performance of
the tasks referred to in Rule 58 of the Regulations under the PCT.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Late payment fee

**[[150.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-150.1 .sectionLabel}** An applicant of an
international application filed with the Commissioner must pay the late
payment fee set out in Rule 58*bis*.2 of the Regulations under the PCT
if an invitation is sent to that applicant by the Commissioner under
Rule 58*bis*.1(a) of the Regulations under the PCT.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2023-113, s. 6](#1401207-1437111){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1401207-1437111.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional fee

**[[151]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-151 .sectionLabel}** The amount of the
additional fee for an international preliminary examination under
Article 34(3)(a) of the Patent Cooperation Treaty is the fee set out in
item 20 of Schedule 2.

### [National Phase]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1183334 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Designated Office

**[[152]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-152 .sectionLabel}** If an international
application in which Canada is designated under the Patent Cooperation
Treaty is filed, the Commissioner must act as the designated Office in
accordance with the Patent Cooperation Treaty, the Regulations under the
PCT and the Administrative Instructions under the PCT.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 54](#1353866-1375377){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353866-1375377.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-152-20191030.html "Link to previous version of section 152"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Elected Office

**[[153]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153 .sectionLabel}** If an international
application in which Canada is designated under the Patent Cooperation
Treaty is filed and the applicant has elected Canada under Article 31 of
the treaty, the Commissioner must act as an elected Office in accordance
with the Patent Cooperation Treaty, the Regulations under the PCT and
the Administrative Instructions under the PCT.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 54](#1353866-1375381){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353866-1375381.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-153-20191030.html "Link to previous version of section 153"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirements

- **[[154]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-154 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  applicant who designates Canada in an international application must,
  not later than 30 months after the priority date,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the International Bureau of the World
    Intellectual Property Organization has not published the
    international application, submit to the Commissioner a copy of the
    international application;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the description contained in the international
    application, other than any sequence listings, is entirely in a
    language other than English or French, submit to the Commissioner an
    English or French translation of the description other than any
    sequence listings;

  - [(b.1)]{.lawlabel} if the claims contained in the international
    application are entirely in a language other than English or French,
    submit to the Commissioner an English or French translation of the
    claims; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} pay the basic national fee, which is

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the small entity fee set out in item 21 of
      Schedule 2, if the small entity status condition set out in
      subsection 44(2) is met and if, not later than 30 months after the
      priority date, a small entity declaration is filed in respect of
      the application in accordance with subsection 44(3), and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the standard fee set out in
      that item.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fee

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An applicant who complies with the requirements of
  subsection (1) after the second anniversary of the international
  filing date must, not later than 30 months after the priority date,
  pay

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the small entity fee set out in item 8 of Schedule
    2 for the second anniversary of the filing date of an application
    for a patent, if the small entity status condition set out in
    subsection 44(2) is met and if, not later than 30 months after the
    priority date, a small entity declaration is filed in respect of the
    application for a patent in accordance with subsection 44(3); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the standard fee set out in that
    item for the second anniversary of the filing date of an application
    for a patent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reinstatement of rights

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An applicant who fails to comply with the
  requirements of subsection (1) and, if applicable, subsection (2) not
  later than 30 months after the priority date, is considered to have
  complied with those requirements within that time if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} not later than 12 months after that time, the
    applicant

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} submits to the Commissioner a request that the
      rights of the applicant be reinstated with respect to that
      international application and a statement that the failure was
      unintentional,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} complies with the requirements of paragraphs
      (1)(a) to (b.1),

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} pays the basic national fee, which is

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} the small entity fee set out in item 21 of
        Schedule 2, if the small entity status condition set out in
        subsection 44(2) is met and if, not later than 12 months after
        the time referred to in subsection (1), a small entity
        declaration is filed in respect of the application in accordance
        with subsection 44(3), and

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the standard fee set out in
        that item, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} pays the fee for reinstatement of rights set out
      in item 22 of Schedule 2; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the applicant complies with the requirements of
    paragraph (a) after the second anniversary of the international
    filing date, the applicant,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} on or before the third anniversary of the
      international filing date but not later than 12 months after the
      time referred to in subsection (1), pays

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} if the small entity status condition set out in
        subsection 44(2) is met and if, on or before that third
        anniversary, a small entity declaration is filed in respect of
        the application for a patent in accordance with subsection
        44(3), the small entity fee set out in item 8 of Schedule 2 for
        the second anniversary of the filing date of an application for
        a patent, and

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the standard fee set out in
        that item for the second anniversary of the filing date of an
        application for a patent, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} after the third anniversary of the international
      filing date but not later than 12 months after the time referred
      to in subsection (1), pays

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} if the small entity status condition set out in
        subsection 44(2) is met and if, not later than 12 months after
        the time referred to in subsection (1), a small entity
        declaration is filed in respect of the application for a patent
        in accordance with subsection 44(3), the small entity fee set
        out in item 8 of Schedule 2 for the second and third anniversary
        of the filing date of an application for a patent, and

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} in any other case, the standard fee set out in
        that item for the second and third anniversary of the filing
        date of an application for a patent.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension in case of attempted payment

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If the applicant of an international application
  fails to comply with the requirements of subsection (1) and, if
  applicable, subsection (2), not later than 30 months after the
  priority date and if, before the end of a period of 12 months after
  that time, the Commissioner receives a communication clearly
  indicating the applicant's intention to pay some or all of the fees
  required by subsection (3), but all of the fees required by
  subsection (3) are not paid before the end of that 12-month period,
  those fees are considered to have been paid on the day on which that
  communication is received if the unpaid fees, together with the
  additional fee for late payment set out in item 23 of Schedule 2, are
  paid after the end of that 12-month period but not later than two
  months after the day on which the communication is received.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2024-241, s. 18\]]{.Repealed}

- [(5.1)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2024-241, s. 18\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Correction of error -- naming of
  applicants

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The Commissioner must, on request, correct an error
  in the records of the Patent Office with respect to the naming of
  applicants in respect of a PCT national phase application if the
  request contains a statement to the effect that the error arose from
  inadvertence, accident or mistake, without any fraudulent or deceptive
  intention, and is made by the person who paid the basic national fee
  prescribed by paragraph (1)(c) or subparagraph (3)(a)(iii) not later
  than the earlier of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the later of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the day on which a period of three months after
      the national phase entry date of that application expires, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the Commissioner sends a notice under
      subsection (7) before the end of a period of three months after
      the national phase entry date of that application, the day on
      which a period of three months after the date of the notice
      expires, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the Commissioner records a transfer of the
    application for a patent under section 49 of the Act, on or before
    the day on which the Commissioner received the request to record
    that transfer.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} If the Commissioner has reasonable grounds to believe
  that the person who complied with the requirements of subsection (1)
  and, if applicable, subsection (2), is neither the applicant of the
  international application nor their legal representative, the
  Commissioner must by notice require that person to establish that they
  are either the applicant of the international application or their
  legal representative.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Person considered never to have
  complied

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} If the person who complied with the requirements of
  subsection (1) and, if applicable, subsection (2), fails to comply
  with the notice not later than three months after the date of the
  notice, that person is considered never to have complied with those
  requirements.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of subsection 3(1)

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} Subsection 3(1) does not apply in respect of the
  times referred to in subsection (1), (2), (3), (4) or (6).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception to subsection 3(1)

  [(10)]{.lawlabel} Subsection 3(1) does not authorize the Commissioner
  to extend the time referred to in subsection (8) for complying with
  the notice beyond the later of the end of a period of six months after
  the date of the notice and the end of a period of 30 months after the
  priority date.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of Article 48(2) of
  Patent Cooperation Treaty

  [(11)]{.lawlabel} Article 48(2) of the Patent Cooperation Treaty does
  not apply in respect of the times referred to in subsection (1),
  (2), (3) or (4) of this section or in respect of any time limit
  applicable to a PCT national phase application.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of certain Rules under
  the PCT

  [(12)]{.lawlabel} Rules 49*ter*.1(f) and 49*ter*.2 of the Regulations
  under the PCT do not apply to a PCT national phase application.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}New PCT national phase application

  [(13)]{.lawlabel} Once an international application becomes a PCT
  national phase application, it may not become a new PCT national phase
  application unless the earlier PCT national phase application is
  withdrawn.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of Canadian legislation

- **[[155]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-155 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  an international application becomes a PCT national phase application,
  the application is considered, beginning on its national phase entry
  date, to be an application for a patent filed in Canada and, subject
  to sections 157 to 163, the Act and these Rules apply beginning on
  that date in respect of that application.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}National phase entry date

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subject to section 210, the national phase entry date
  of an application for a patent is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the applicant has not complied with the
    applicable requirements of subsection 154(3), the day on which the
    applicant complied with the requirements of subsection 154(1) and,
    if applicable, subsection 154(2) or, if the applicant complied with
    those requirements on different days, the latest of those days; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the applicant has complied with the applicable
    requirements of subsection 154(3), the day on which the applicant
    complied with those requirements or, if the applicant complied with
    those requirements on different days, the latest of those days.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Small entity declaration

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of subsection (2), an applicant is
  not considered to have paid the fee referred to in subparagraph
  154(1)(c)(i), paragraph 154(2)(a) or clause 154(3)(a)(iii)(A) or
  154(3)(b)(i)(A) or (ii)(A) until the small entity declaration has been
  filed.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2022-120, s. 33\]]{.Repealed}

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2022-120, s. 33\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 33](#1353815-1375149){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353815-1375149.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-155-20191030.html "Link to previous version of section 155"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Translation --- part of description or
claims

- **[[155.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-155.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} At or before the time that an
  international application becomes a PCT national phase application,
  the applicant must submit to the Commissioner a translation of any
  part of the description --- other than any sequence listings --- and
  any part of the claims that are in a language other than English or
  French.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Translation --- abstract, request or
  statement

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} At or before the time that an international
  application becomes a PCT national phase application, the applicant
  must submit to the Commissioner a translation of any of the following
  elements, or any part of the following elements, that are in a
  language other than English or French:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any language-dependent free text contained in a
    sequence listing that does not also appear in English or French;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any text matter in a drawing;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the abstract;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the request referred to in article 4 of the Patent
    Cooperation Treaty, except if the International Bureau of the World
    Intellectual Property Organization has published the international
    application on or before the national phase entry date; and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} any statement made under Article 19 of the Patent
    Cooperation Treaty.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 34](#1353816-1375161){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353816-1375161.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Error in translation --- notice

- **[[155.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-155.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If the Commissioner, before a
  request for examination is made, or an examiner, in the course of
  examining an application for a patent, has reasonable grounds to
  believe that a translation submitted under paragraph 154(1)(b) or
  (b.1) or section 155.1 contains an error, the Commissioner or the
  examiner, as the case may be, must by notice inform the applicant of
  the error.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Error in translation --- correction

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An applicant may correct a translation submitted
  under paragraph 154(1)(b) or (b.1) or section 155.1 that contains an
  error by submitting the following to the Commissioner before the day
  on which a notice of allowance or a conditional notice of allowance is
  sent or, if that notice is withdrawn by the Commissioner or set aside
  in accordance with subsection 85.1(4), before the day on which a
  notice of allowance or a conditional notice of allowance is sent
  again:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the corrected translation;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a request that the corrected translation replace
    the original translation; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a statement to the effect that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} at the time the original translation was
      submitted, it would have been obvious to a skilled translator
      fluent in both the original language and the language of
      translation that the original translation contained an error and
      that the corrected translation is an accurate translation,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the error in the original translation occurred
      despite due care having been taken in its preparation, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the request is being made within a reasonable
      time after the applicant became aware of the error.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Date of replacement

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (4), a corrected translation
  submitted in accordance with subsection (2) replaces the original
  translation and is considered to have been submitted on the day on
  which the original translation was submitted.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Open to public inspection --- date

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection 55(2) of the Act, if a
  corrected translation of any part of the specification contained in an
  application for a patent is submitted under subsection (2) after the
  application became open to public inspection, in English or French,
  under section 10 of the Act, the specification is considered to have
  become open to public inspection, in English or French, on the day on
  which the applicant submits the corrected translation or, if more than
  one corrected translation of the specification is submitted, on the
  last day on which a corrected translation is submitted.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 34](#1353816-1375178){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353816-1375178.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction --- translation of
specification or drawings

**[[155.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-155.3 .sectionLabel}** A translation or
corrected translation, submitted under section 155.1 or subsection
155.2(2), respectively, of all or part of the text matter in a
specification or a drawing must not contain any matter not reasonably to
be inferred from the specification or the drawings contained in the
application on its filing date.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 34](#1353816-1375182){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353816-1375182.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Complete copy

- **[[155.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-155.4
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An applicant who submits a
  translation under section 155.1 or a corrected translation under
  subsection 155.2(2) must, at the same time, submit the following to
  the Commissioner:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a translation referred to in
    subsection 155.1(1) or a correction to that translation, a complete
    copy of the description --- other than any sequence listings --- or
    the claims, as the case may be, that contains the translated text
    matter and the text matter that already appeared in English or
    French;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a translation referred to in
    paragraph 155.1(2)(a) or a correction to that translation, a
    complete copy of the sequence listing that contains the translated
    text matter and the text matter that already appeared in English or
    French;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a translation referred to in
    paragraph 155.1(2)(b) or a correction to that translation, a
    complete copy of the drawing that contains the translated text
    matter and the text matter that already appeared in English or
    French; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a translation referred to in
    paragraph 155.1(2)(c), (d) or (e) or a correction to that
    translation, a complete copy of the abstract, request or statement,
    as the case may be, that contains the translated text matter and the
    text matter that already appeared in English or French.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Integrated translation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of section 155.1 and subsection
  155.2(2), if a complete copy is submitted in accordance with
  subsection (1), the applicant need not submit the translation or
  corrected translation as a separate document.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 34](#1353816-1375193){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353816-1375193.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Translation replaces original ---
description

- **[[155.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-155.5
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a translation is submitted under
  paragraph 154(1)(b), the translation replaces the entirety of the
  description other than any sequences listings.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Translation replaces original ---
  claims

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a translation is submitted under paragraph
  154(1)(b.1), the translation replaces the entirety of the claims.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Translation replaces original ---
  element of application

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If a translation is submitted under section 155.1,
  the translation replaces the corresponding text matter in the PCT
  national phase application.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Failure to provide translation ---
  certain elements

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If the applicant fails to provide a translation of a
  part of the description or claims or of any text matter in a drawing,
  in accordance with subsection 155.1(1) or paragraph 155.1(2)(b),
  respectively, the corresponding untranslated text matter in the PCT
  national phase application must not be taken into account for the
  purpose of interpreting the scope of protection sought or obtained.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Failure to provide translation ---
  statement

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If the applicant fails to provide a translation of
  all or part of the statement referred to in paragraph 155.1(2)(e) in
  accordance with that paragraph, the statement may be disregarded by
  the Commissioner.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Failure to provide translation or
  complete copy

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} If the applicant fails to provide a translation in
  accordance with paragraph 155.1(2)(c) or (d) or a complete copy in
  accordance with paragraph 155.4(1)(a), (b) or (c), the Commissioner
  may by notice require the applicant to provide the translation or
  complete copy no later than three months after the date of the notice.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 34](#1353816-1375208){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353816-1375208.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amendments to specification and drawings

- **[[155.6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-155.6
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If all or part of the text matter of
  the specification or the drawings contained in an international
  application on its international filing date is in a language other
  than English or French, the specification and the drawings contained
  in the PCT national phase application must not be amended to add
  matter not reasonably to be inferred from both

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the specification or the drawings contained in the
    international application on its international filing date; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the specification or the drawings contained in the
    international application immediately after it became a PCT national
    phase application, excluding any text matter that is not in English
    or French other than text matter in a sequence listing that is not
    language-dependent free text.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sequence listing

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraph (1)(a), if the
  international application contains, on its international filing date,
  text matter in a sequence listing that is both in English or French
  and in a language other than English or French, the text matter that
  is in a language other than English or French is considered not to
  have been included in the application on that date.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Divisional application

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The specification and the drawings contained in a
  divisional application resulting from the division of a PCT national
  phase application must not be amended to add matter that may not be or
  could not have been added --- under subsection 38.2(2) of the Act,
  subsection (1) of this section or this subsection --- to the
  specification and the drawings contained in the application for a
  patent from which the divisional application results.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of subsections (1) and
  (3)

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subsections (1) and (3) do not apply if it is
  admitted in the specification contained in the divisional application
  that the matter is prior art.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 34](#1353816-1375221){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353816-1375221.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Clarification --- divisional application

**[[155.7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-155.7 .sectionLabel}** For greater
certainty, the specification and the drawings contained in a divisional
application resulting from the division of a PCT national phase
application must not contain matter that is not in the specification and
the drawings contained in the original application on its filing date,
or if the original application is itself a divisional application, on
its presentation date, unless

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the matter may be or could have been added --- under
  section 155.6 of these Rules and subsection 38.2(1) and paragraph
  38.2(3.1)(b) of the Act, without taking into account subsection
  155.6(4) of these Rules or subsection 38.2(4) of the Act --- to the
  specification and the drawings contained in the original application;
  or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} it is admitted in the specification contained in the
  divisional application that the matter is prior art.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 34](#1353816-1375227){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353816-1375227.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Clarification

- **[[156]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-156 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  greater certainty, in respect of an international application that has
  become a PCT national phase application, for the purposes of the Act
  and these Rules,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} documents or information included in the
    international application as filed are deemed to have been received
    by the Commissioner on the international filing date; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} documents or information, other than the documents
    or information referred to in paragraph (a), furnished in accordance
    with the requirements of the Patent Cooperation Treaty before the
    application became a PCT national phase application are deemed to
    have been received by the Commissioner on the day on which they were
    so furnished.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- sequence listings

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Paragraph (1)(b) does not apply in respect of
  sequence listings that do not form part of the international
  application.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application considered open to public
inspection

**[[157]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-157 .sectionLabel}** If an international
application is published in English or French by the International
Bureau of the World Intellectual Property Organization under Article 21
of the Patent Cooperation Treaty on or before its national phase entry
date, the application is considered to be open to public inspection
under section 10 of the Act beginning on the date of that publication.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of subsection 27(2) of
Act

**[[158]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158 .sectionLabel}** The requirements of
subsection 27(2) of the Act regarding the petition and the application
fee do not apply in respect of a PCT national phase application.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of certain provisions of
Act

- **[[159]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-159
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subsection 27(7), sections 27.01, 28
  and 28.01, subsections 38.2(3) and 78.1(2) and section 78.2 of the Act
  do not apply in respect of a PCT national phase application.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of paragraph
  38.2(3.1)(a) of Act

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Paragraph 38.2(3.1)(a) of the Act does not apply in
  respect of a divisional application resulting from the division of a
  PCT national phase application.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 35](#1353818-1375234){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353818-1375234.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-159-20191030.html "Link to previous version of section 159"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of section 78 of Act

**[[160]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-160 .sectionLabel}** Section 78 of the Act
does not apply in respect of a time period referred to in that section
that ends before the national phase entry date of a PCT national phase
application.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 35](#1353818-1375238){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353818-1375238.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-160-20191030.html "Link to previous version of section 160"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Filing date

**[[161]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-161 .sectionLabel}** The filing date of a
PCT national phase application is the international filing date.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Filing date deemed to be within 12 months

**[[162]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-162 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
paragraph 28.1(1)(b) of the Act and subparagraphs 28.2(1)(d)(iii) and
28.4(5)(a)(i) and (ii) of the Act, even if the conditions referred to in
paragraph 28.4(6)(b) of the Act are not met, the filing date of the
pending application or the co-pending application, as the case may be,
shall be deemed to be within 12 months after the filing date of the
previously regularly filed application if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the filing date of the pending application or the
  co-pending application, as the case may be, is more than 12 months
  after the filing date of the previously regularly filed application,
  but within two months after the end of those 12 months;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the pending application or the co-pending
  application, as the case may be, is a PCT national phase application
  or a divisional application resulting from the division of a PCT
  national phase application; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the right of priority, in respect of the previously
  regularly filed application, was restored under Rule 26*bis*.3 of the
  Regulations under the PCT and that restoration is, under Rule
  49*ter*.1 of those Regulations, effective in Canada.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application considered not to be referred
to in paragraph 28.2(1)(c) or (d) of Act

**[[163]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-163 .sectionLabel}** An international
application is considered not to be an application for a patent referred
to in paragraph 28.2(1)(c) of the Act or not to be a co-pending
application referred to in paragraph 28.2(1)(d) of the Act, unless it
has become a PCT national phase application.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Patent not invalid

**[[164]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-164 .sectionLabel}** A patent that was
granted on the basis of an international application must not be
declared invalid by reason only that a fee referred to in section 154
was not paid.

## [PART 3]{.HLabel1}[Transitional Provisions]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1183435 .Part}

### [DIVISION 1]{.HLabel2}[Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1183436 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[165]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-165 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  following definitions apply in this Part.

  [[application preceding the coming-into-force date]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[application preceding the coming-into-force
      date]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2022-120, s.
      36\]]{.Repealed}

  [[category 1 application]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[category 1 application]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an
      application for a patent for which the filing date is before
      October 1, 1989. ([demande de catégorie 1]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[category 2 application]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[category 2 application]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/2022-120, s. 36\]]{.Repealed}

  [[category 3 application]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[category 3 application]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an
      application for a patent for which the filing date is on or after
      October 1, 1996 but before October 30, 2019. ([demande de
      catégorie 3]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[coming-into-force date]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[coming-into-force date]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/2022-120, s. 36\]]{.Repealed}

  [[former Rules]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[former Rules]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the [Patent
      Rules](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-423) as they read immediately
      before October 30, 2019. ([anciennes règles]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interpretation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the filing date of an application for a patent,
  other than a PCT national phase application, as determined in
  accordance with section 78.2 of the Act, precedes October 30, 2019,
  the filing date of that application is, for the purposes of these
  Rules, the filing date determined in accordance with that section.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 36](#1353820-1375243){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353820-1375243.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 55](#1353871-1375386){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353871-1375386.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-165-20191030.html "Link to previous version of section 165"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reissued patents

**[[166]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-166 .sectionLabel}** For greater certainty,
for the purposes of this Part, a reissued patent is considered to be
issued on the basis of the original application.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of subsection 3(1)

**[[167]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-167 .sectionLabel}** For greater certainty,
subsection 3(1) applies in respect of any time limit fixed by the former
Rules that continues to apply under these Rules.

### [DIVISION 2]{.HLabel2}[Rules in Respect of Category 1 Applications]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1183452 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of certain provisions of
these Rules

- **[[168]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-168
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Sections 14, 47 to 51, 55, 56, 58 to
  63, 65 and 74, subsection 86(1.1), subparagraphs 87(1)(a)(iii) and
  (b)(iii), sections 93 to 96, subsections 97(2) and (3) and sections 98
  and 104 do not apply in respect of a category 1 application.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of subsection 86(14)

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection 86(14) does not apply in respect of a
  category 1 application at any time at which it is forfeited under
  subsection 73(1) of the Act, as it read immediately before October 1,
  1989.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2022-120, s. 37\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 37](#1353821-1375247){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353821-1375247.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-168-20191030.html "Link to previous version of section 168"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of certain provisions of
former Rules

- **[[169]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-169
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (3), sections
  170, 171, 174, 175, 177, 181 and 183 to 186 of the former Rules
  continue to apply in respect of a category 1 application.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of section 32 of former
  Rules

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Section 32 of the former Rules continues to apply in
  respect of a category 1 application for which a notice of allowance
  was sent before October 30, 2019, other than such an application that
  is forfeited under subsection 73(1) of the Act, as it read immediately
  before October 1, 1989, and is subsequently restored.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Photographs

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Paragraphs 177(1)(a), (b), (e) and (h) of the former
  Rules do not apply in respect of photographs provided in respect of a
  category 1 application.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of section 179 of former
  Rules

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Section 179 of the former Rules continues to apply in
  respect of a category 1 application, except that the reference to "the
  filing date and number of each application in a country other than
  Canada on which the applicant bases the claim" in that section is to
  be read as a reference to "the filing date and the name of the country
  of filing of each application that was filed in a country other than
  Canada on which the claim is based".

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 55](#1353871-1375390){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353871-1375390.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-169-20191030.html "Link to previous version of section 169"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reference to "the Act"

**[[170]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-170 .sectionLabel}** A reference in section
42 and subsection 86(13) to "the Act", in relation to a category 1
application, is to be read as a reference to "the Act as it read
immediately before October 1, 1989".

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Final fee

**[[171]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-171 .sectionLabel}** With respect to a
category 1 application,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the reference to "the final fee set out in item 14 of
  Schedule 2 not later than four months after the date of the notice" in
  subsections 86(1), (6), (10) and (12) is to be read as a reference to
  "the final fee set out in paragraph 6(b) of Schedule II to the former
  Rules within six months after the date of the notice"; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the reference to "the small entity fee set out in
  paragraph 14(a) of Schedule 2", "the standard fee set out in paragraph
  14(a) of Schedule 2" and "the fee set out in paragraph 14(b) of
  Schedule 2" in subsection 87(1) is to be read as a reference to "the
  small entity fee set out in subparagraph 6(b)(i) of Schedule II to the
  former Rules", "the standard fee set out in paragraph 6(b)(i) of
  Schedule II to the former Rules" and "the fee set out in subparagraph
  6(b)(ii) of Schedule 2 to the former Rules", respectively.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 38](#1353825-1375250){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353825-1375250.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 53](#1353858-1375352){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353858-1375352.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-171-20191030.html "Link to previous version of section 171"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rejection for defects

- **[[172]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-172 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  an applicant of a category 1 application replies in good faith to a
  requisition made under subsection 30(2) of the former Rules, on or
  before the date set out in subsection (4) of this section, but the
  examiner, after receiving the reply, has reasonable grounds to believe
  that the application does not comply with the Act or these Rules in
  respect of any of the defects referred to in the requisition and that
  the applicant will not amend the application to comply with the Act
  and these Rules, the examiner may reject the application.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice --- application found allowable
  after final action

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If an applicant of a category 1 application replies
  in good faith to a requisition made under subsection 30(4) of the
  former Rules, on or before the date set out in subsection (4) of this
  section, if the examiner has reasonable grounds to believe that the
  application complies with the Act and these Rules and if the
  Commissioner has not sent a notice of allowance under subsection 30(5)
  of the former Rules, the Commissioner must by notice inform the
  applicant that the rejection is withdrawn and the application has been
  found to be allowable and must require the payment of the final fee
  set out in paragraph 6(b) of Schedule II to the former Rules not later
  than four months after the date of that notice.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rejection not withdrawn after final
  action

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If an applicant of a category 1 application replies
  in good faith to a requisition made under subsection 30(4) of the
  former Rules, on or before the date set out in subsection (4) of this
  section, but the examiner, after that date, still has reasonable
  grounds to believe that the application does not comply with the Act
  or these Rules,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if a notice was not sent under paragraph 30(6)(a)
    of the former Rules, the Commissioner must by notice inform the
    applicant that the rejection has not been withdrawn;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any amendments made during the period beginning on
    the date of the final action notice and ending on the date set out
    in subsection (4) of this section are considered never to have been
    made; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the application must be reviewed by the
    Commissioner.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Date

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsections (1) to (3), the date
  is the later of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the day that is six months after the date of the
    requisition referred to in subsection 30(2) or (4) of the former
    Rules, as applicable, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the application is deemed to be abandoned under
    subsection 30(1) of the Act, as it read immediately before October
    1, 1989, for the failure of the applicant to prosecute the
    application following the requisition made under subsection 30(2)
    or (4) of the former Rules, as applicable, the day on which the
    conditions for reinstatement set out in subsection 30(2) of the Act,
    as it read immediately before October 1, 1989, are met in respect of
    that abandonment.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice --- application found allowable
  after amendments

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If the applicant of a category 1 application has
  complied with a notice sent under subsection 30(6.3) of the former
  Rules in respect of amendments that have to be made and if a notice of
  allowance was not sent under that subsection, the Commissioner must by
  notice inform the applicant that the rejection is withdrawn and the
  application has been found to be allowable and require the payment of
  the final fee set out in paragraph 6(b) of Schedule II to the former
  Rules not later than four months after the date of the notice sent
  under this subsection.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of paragraph (3)(b)

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Paragraph (3)(b) does not apply in respect of an
  application for a patent that, before December 29, 2013, was rejected
  by an examiner under subsection 30(3) of the former Rules unless the
  rejection has been withdrawn.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of subsection 3(1)

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} Subsection 3(1) does not apply in respect of the time
  referred to in subsection (2) or (5).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No amendment after rejection

**[[173]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-173 .sectionLabel}** If a category 1
application is rejected by an examiner under subsection 172(1) of these
Rules or subsection 30(3) of the former Rules, the specification and the
drawings contained in the application must not be amended after the date
prescribed by subsection 172(4) of these Rules, unless

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a notice is sent to the applicant informing them that
  the rejection is withdrawn;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the amendments made are those required in a notice
  sent under subsection 86(11) of these Rules or subsection 30(6.3) of
  the former Rules; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the Supreme Court of Canada, the Federal Court of
  Appeal or the Federal Court orders the amendments to be made.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reference

**[[174]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-174 .sectionLabel}** The reference to "may
be or could have been added, under section 38.2 of the Act without
taking into account subsection 38.2(4) of the Act, to the specification
and the drawings contained in the original application" in paragraph
91(a) is to be read, in relation to a category 1 application, as a
reference to "results from an amendment to the specification or drawings
that is not prohibited by section 181 of the former Rules".

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reference to subsection 28.4(2) of Act
and paragraph 93(1)(b) of these Rules

**[[175]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-175 .sectionLabel}** The reference to
"subsection 28.4(2) of the Act" in paragraph 92(c) and the reference to
"paragraph 93(1)(b)" in paragraph 92(f), in relation to a category 1
application, are to be read as a reference to "section 179 of the former
Rules" and "subsection 184(3) of the former Rules", respectively.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional fee for restoration of
application

**[[176]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-176 .sectionLabel}** The additional fee to
be paid for the restoration of a category 1 application that was
forfeited under subsection 73(1) of the Act, as it read immediately
before October 1, 1989, is \$200.

### [DIVISION 4]{.HLabel2}[Rules in Respect of Category 3 Applications]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1183611 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of certain provisions of
these Rules

- **[[188]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-188
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subsections 73(1) and (4), 81(1),
  84(2) and 103(2) and section 132 do not apply in respect of a category
  3 application.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of section 74

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Section 74 does not apply in respect of a request for
  priority made in relation to a category 3 application before October
  30, 2019.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2022-120, s. 40\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of subsection 103(1)

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subsection 103(1) does not apply in respect of a
  category 3 application that has a presentation date before October 30,
  2019.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of section 104

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Section 104 does not apply in relation to a category
  3 application in respect of which an assignment was registered under
  subsection 49(2) of the Act, as it read immediately before October 30,
  2019.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 40](#1353828-1375265){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353828-1375265.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 55](#1353871-1375397){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353871-1375397.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-188-20191030.html "Link to previous version of section 188"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of section 26.1 of former
Rules

- **[[189]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-189
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Section 26.1 of the former Rules
  continues to apply in respect of the time prescribed by section 98 of
  the former Rules in respect of a category 3 application.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of section 32 of former
  Rules

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Section 32 of the former Rules continues to apply in
  respect of a category 3 application for which a notice of allowance
  was sent before October 30, 2019 other than such an application that
  is deemed to be abandoned under paragraph 73(1)(f) of the Act, as it
  read immediately before October 30, 2019, and is subsequently
  reinstated.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of section 66 of the former
  Rules

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Section 66 of the former Rules continues to apply in
  relation to a category 3 application in respect of which the
  applicant, before October 30, 2019, complied with the requirements of
  subsection 58(1) of the former Rules and, if applicable, with the
  requirements of subsection 58(2) of those Rules.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of section 98 of former
  Rules

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If, under section 78.51 or 78.52 of the Act, section
  73 of the Act, as it read immediately before October 30, 2019, applies
  in respect of the abandonment of a category 3 application, section 98
  of the former Rules continues to apply in respect of that abandonment.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 55](#1353871-1375405){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353871-1375405.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-189-20191030.html "Link to previous version of section 189"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension of time

**[[190]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-190 .sectionLabel}** The Commissioner is
authorized to extend, in respect of a category 3 application, the time
referred to in the former Rules for payment of the fee referred to in
subsection 3(3), (5) or (7) of the former Rules or the time referred to
in subsection 199(2) or (5) of these Rules for the payment of the final
fee, after the expiry of that time, if the Commissioner considers that
the circumstances justify the extension and if the conditions referred
to in subsection 3(3) of these Rules are met.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 41](#1353829-1375269){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353829-1375269.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-190-20191030.html "Link to previous version of section 190"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prescribed date --- withdrawal of request
for priority

**[[191]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-191 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
subsection 10(4) of the Act, if a request for priority that was made in
respect of a category 3 application has been withdrawn with respect to a
previously regularly filed application for a patent before October 30,
2019, the prescribed date is, despite section 17, the later of

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the earlier of

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the day on which a period of 16 months after the
    filing date of the previously regularly filed application expires,
    and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the request for priority is based on more than
    one previously regularly filed application, the day on which a
    period of 16 months after the earliest of the filing dates of those
    applications expires, and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the Commissioner is able to stop the technical
  preparations to open the category 3 application to public inspection
  before the expiry of the confidentiality period referred to in
  subsection 10(2) of the Act, the day on which the Commissioner stops
  those preparations.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 55](#1353871-1375408){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353871-1375408.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-191-20191030.html "Link to previous version of section 191"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prescribed date --- withdrawn application

**[[192]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-192 .sectionLabel}** If a category 3
application is withdrawn before October 30, 2019, for the purposes of
subsection 10(5) of the Act, the prescribed date is, despite section 18
of these Rules, the day that is two months before the expiry date of the
confidentiality period referred to in subsection 10(2) of the Act or, if
the Commissioner is able to stop the technical preparations to open the
application to public inspection at a subsequent date preceding the
expiry of that period, that subsequent date.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 55](#1353871-1375412){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353871-1375412.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-192-20191030.html "Link to previous version of section 192"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception to subsection 50(1)

**[[193]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-193 .sectionLabel}** In respect of a
category 3 application, the applicant may comply with the requirements
of subsection 73(1) of the former Rules instead of the requirements of
subsection 50(1) of these Rules.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception to section 58

**[[194]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-194 .sectionLabel}** In respect of a
category 3 application for which the filing date was before June 2,
2007, the applicant may comply with the requirements of sections 111 to
131 of the [Patent Rules](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-423), as they read
immediately before June 2, 2007, instead of the requirements of section
58 of these Rules.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirements for request for priority

- **[[195]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-195 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of subsection 28.4(2) of the Act, a request for priority
  in respect of a category 3 application must be made in the petition of
  that application or in a separate document, before the later of the
  end of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a period 16 months after the earliest of the filing
    dates of the previously regularly filed applications for a patent on
    which the request is based, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a period four months after the filing date of the
    category 3 application.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Time prescribed by subsection 73(1)

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} With respect to a request for priority made in
  relation to a category 3 application, the reference to "the time
  prescribed by subsection (1)" in subsections 73(2) and (6) is to be
  read as a reference to "the time prescribed by subsection 195(1)".

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Correction --- error in filing date

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An error in a filing date submitted under subsection
  28.4(2) of the Act in relation to a request for priority in respect of
  a category 3 application may be corrected on request submitted before
  the earliest of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the end of the period referred to in subsection
    (1), as determined using the corrected filing date,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the end of the period referred to in subsection
    (1), as determined using the uncorrected filing date, and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, the day on which the applicant
    submits their approval, under subsection 10(2) of the Act, for the
    category 3 application to be open to public inspection before the
    expiry of the confidentiality period, unless that approval is
    withdrawn in time to permit the Commissioner to stop the technical
    preparations to open the application to public inspection.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of subsection 3(1)

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subsection 3(1) does not apply to the time referred
  to in subsection (1).

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice requiring application to be made
accessible

- **[[196]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-196 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If,
  for the purpose of examining a pending category 3 application in
  respect of which a request for priority was made before October 30,
  2019, the examiner takes into account a previously regularly filed
  application for a patent --- other than such an application that was
  filed in Canada --- on which the request for priority is based, the
  examiner may by notice require the applicant of the pending
  application to do one of the following not later than four months
  after the date of the notice:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to submit to the Commissioner a copy of that
    previously regularly filed application, certified by the patent
    office where it was filed, as well as a certificate from that office
    indicating the filing date; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to make a copy of that previously regularly filed
    application available to the Commissioner in a digital library that
    is specified by the Commissioner as being accepted for that purpose,
    and inform the Commissioner that it is so available.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Request for priority considered
  withdrawn

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the applicant fails to comply with the
  requirements of paragraph (1)(a) or (b) in respect of a previously
  regularly filed application for a patent not later than four months
  after the date of the notice referred to in subsection (1), the
  request for priority is considered to have been withdrawn at the end
  of that time in respect of that previously regularly filed application
  unless, before the end of that time,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a request is made to the patent office where the
    previously regularly filed application was filed to provide the copy
    and certificate referred to in paragraph (1)(a); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the applicant submits to the Commissioner a request
    that the Commissioner restore the right of priority on the basis of
    that previously regularly filed application and a statement
    indicating the patent office to which the request referred to in
    paragraph (a) was made and the date of that request.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Applicant considered to comply

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the conditions set out in paragraphs (2)(a)
  and (b) are met by the applicant in respect of a previously regularly
  filed application for a patent, the applicant is considered to have
  complied with subsection (1) in respect of that application.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Submission of copy and certificate

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If the conditions set out in paragraphs (2)(a)
  and (b) are met in respect of a previously regularly filed application
  for a patent and if the patent office where that application was filed
  provides the copy and certificate referred to in paragraph (1)(a), the
  applicant or the patentee, as applicable, must submit the copy and
  certificate to the Commissioner not later than three months after the
  day on which they were provided.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Request for priority considered
  withdrawn

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If the applicant or patentee fails to comply with
  subsection (4) in respect of a previously regularly filed application
  for a patent, the request for priority is considered to have been
  withdrawn at the end of the time referred to in that subsection in
  respect of that application.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Subsections (1) to (5) do not apply in respect of a
  previously regularly filed application on the basis of which an
  applicant requests priority if the pending category 3 application is a
  PCT national phase application or a divisional application resulting
  from the division of a PCT national phase application and if the
  requirements of Rule 17.1(a), (b) or (b-*bis*) of the Regulations
  under the PCT are complied with in respect of that previously
  regularly filed application.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 55](#1353871-1375415){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353871-1375415.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-196-20191030.html "Link to previous version of section 196"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prescribed time --- subsection 35(2) of
Act

- **[[197]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-197 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of subsection 35(2) of the Act, in respect of a category
  3 application, the prescribed time for making a request for
  examination and for paying the fee is any time before

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of such an application that is not a
    divisional application, the end of a period of five years after the
    filing date of the application; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of such an application that is a
    divisional application, the later of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the end of the time that is applicable under this
      subsection in respect of the original application, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the end of a period of three months after the
      presentation date of the divisional application or, if the
      presentation date precedes October 30, 2019, the end of a period
      of six months after the presentation date of the divisional
      application.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of subsection 3(1)

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection 3(1) does not apply to the times
  prescribed by subsection (1).

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 55](#1353871-1375418){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353871-1375418.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-197-20191030.html "Link to previous version of section 197"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception to subsection 84(1)

**[[198]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-198 .sectionLabel}** If a request is made by
an applicant under subsection 84(1) in relation to a category 3
application, the Commissioner must not, despite that subsection, advance
the examination of that application out of its routine order or, if the
examination has been advanced, must return it to its routine order if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the Commissioner has, under subsection 3(1), extended
  the time fixed for doing anything in respect of the application;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} after April 30, 2011, the Commissioner has, under
  subsection 26(1) of the former Rules, extended the time fixed for
  doing anything in respect of the application;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} after April 30, 2011, the application is or was
  deemed to be abandoned under

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} subsection 73(1) of the Act, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} subsection 73(1) of the Act, as it read
    immediately before October 30, 2019; or

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} on or after October 30, 2019, the application is or
  was deemed to be abandoned under subsection 73(2) of the Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 55](#1353871-1375422){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353871-1375422.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-198-20191030.html "Link to previous version of section 198"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception to subsection 86(16)

**[[198.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-198.1 .sectionLabel}** In subsection
86(16), the reference to paragraph 132(1)(g), in relation to a category
3 application, is to be read as a reference to paragraph 203(1)(e).

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2023-113, s. 8](#1401211-1402221){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1401211-1402221.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rejection for defects

- **[[199]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-199 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  an applicant of a category 3 application replies in good faith to a
  requisition made under subsection 30(2) of the former Rules on or
  before the date set out in subsection (4) of this section but the
  examiner, after receiving the reply, has reasonable grounds to believe
  that the application does not comply with the Act or these Rules in
  respect of any of the defects referred to in the requisition and that
  the applicant will not amend the application to comply with the Act
  and these Rules, the examiner may reject the application.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice --- application found allowable
  after final action

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If an applicant of a category 3 application replies
  in good faith to a requisition made under subsection 30(4) of the
  former Rules, on or before the date set out in subsection (4) of this
  section, and the examiner has reasonable grounds to believe that the
  application complies with the Act and these Rules and if the
  Commissioner has not sent a notice of allowance under subsection 30(5)
  of the former Rules, the Commissioner must by notice inform the
  applicant that the rejection is withdrawn and the application has been
  found to be allowable and require the payment of the final fee set out
  in item 14 of Schedule 2 not later than four months after the date of
  notice sent under this subsection.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rejection not withdrawn after final
  action

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If an applicant of a category 3 application replies
  in good faith to a requisition made under subsection 30(4) of the
  former Rules on or before the date set out in subsection (4) of this
  section but the examiner, after that date, still has reasonable
  grounds to believe that the application does not comply with the Act
  or these Rules,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if a notice was not sent under paragraph 30(6)(a)
    of the former Rules, the Commissioner must by notice inform the
    applicant that the rejection has not been withdrawn;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any amendments made to that application during the
    period beginning on the date of the final action notice and ending
    on the date set out in subsection (4) of this section are considered
    never to have been made; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the application must be reviewed by the
    Commissioner.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Date

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsections (1) to (3), the date
  is the later of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the earlier of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the day that is six months after the date of the
      requisition referred to in subsection 30(2) or (4) of the former
      Rules, as applicable, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the last day of the period determined by the
      Commissioner, if any, under paragraph 73(1)(a) of the Act, as it
      read immediately before October 30, 2019, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the category 3 application is deemed to be
    abandoned under paragraph 73(1)(a) of the Act, as it read
    immediately before October 30, 2019, for failure to reply in good
    faith to a requisition made under subsection 30(2) or (4) of the
    former Rules, as applicable, the day on which the conditions for
    reinstatement set out in subsection 73(3) of the Act, as it read
    immediately before October 30, 2019, are met in respect of that
    abandonment.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice --- application found allowable
  after amendments

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If the applicant of a category 3 application has
  complied with a notice sent under subsection 30(6.3) of the former
  Rules in respect of specific amendments that have to be made and if a
  notice of allowance was not sent under that subsection, the
  Commissioner must by notice inform the applicant that the rejection is
  withdrawn and that the application has been found to be allowable and
  require the payment of the final fee set out in item 14 of Schedule 2
  not later than four months after the date of notice sent under this
  subsection.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of paragraph (3)(b)

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Paragraph (3)(b) does not apply in respect of an
  application for a patent that, before December 29, 2013, was rejected
  by an examiner under subsection 30(3) of the former Rules unless the
  rejection has been withdrawn.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of subsection 3(1)

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} Subsection 3(1) does not apply in respect of the time
  referred to in subsection (2) or (5).

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 53](#1353858-1375358){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353858-1375358.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 55](#1353871-1375425){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353871-1375425.html"
  role="button"}
:::
