Version](section-199-20191030.html "Link to previous version of section 199"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No amendment after rejection

**[[200]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-200 .sectionLabel}** If a category 3
application is rejected by an examiner under subsection 199(1) of these
Rules or subsection 30(3) of the former Rules, the specification and the
drawings contained in the application must not be amended after the date
prescribed by subsection 199(4) of these Rules, unless

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a notice is sent to the applicant informing them that
  the rejection is withdrawn;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the amendments are those required in a notice sent
  under subsection 86(11) of these Rules or subsection 30(6.3) of the
  former Rules; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the Supreme Court of Canada, the Federal Court of
  Appeal or the Federal Court orders the amendments to be made.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Request to furnish sample to independent
expert

- **[[201]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-201 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  notice referred to in subsection 104(4) of the former Rules that was
  filed in respect of a category 3 application is considered to be a
  request referred to in section 95 of these Rules.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Independent expert considered to be
  nominated

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An independent expert who was nominated under
  subsection 109(1) of the former Rules is considered to be nominated
  under section 96 of these Rules.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Documents and information --- divisional
application

- **[[202]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-202 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of paragraph 38.2(3.1)(b) of the Act, the prescribed
  documents and information in respect of a category 3 application that
  has a presentation date before October 30, 2019 are

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of such an application that has a
    presentation date before June 2, 2007, the documents and information
    referred to in clauses 78.2(a)(iii)(A) to (D) of the Act; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of such an application that has a
    presentation date on or after June 2, 2007 but before October 30,
    2019, the documents and information referred to in clauses
    78.2(a)(iv)(A) to (D) of the Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Presentation date

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The presentation date of a category 3 application is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if all of the elements referred to in clauses
    78.2(a)(iii)(A) to (E) of the Act were received by the Commissioner
    before June 2, 2007, the day on which they were received or, if they
    were received on different days, the latest of those days;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if paragraph (a) does not apply and if at least one
    of the elements referred to in clauses 78.2(a)(iv)(A) to (E) of the
    Act was received by the Commissioner on or after June 2, 2007 and
    all of those elements were received by the Commissioner before
    October 30, 2019, the day on which they were received, or if they
    were received on different days, the latest of those days; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if paragraphs (a) and (b) do not apply and if all
    of the documents and information referred to in subsection 103(1) of
    these Rules were received by the Commissioner and at least one of
    those elements was received by the Commissioner on or after October
    30, 2019, the day on which they were received or, if they were
    received on different days, the latest of those days.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 55](#1353871-1375431){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353871-1375431.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-202-20191030.html "Link to previous version of section 202"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application deemed abandoned

- **[[203]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-203 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of subsection 73(2) of the Act, a category 3 application
  is deemed to be abandoned if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a notice is sent under section 31 and the
    requirements are not complied with within the applicable time
    referred to in that section;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the applicant does not reply in good faith to a
    request of the Commissioner for further drawings under subsection
    27(5.2) of the Act not later than three months after the date of the
    request;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the applicant does not reply in good faith to a
    notice of the Commissioner referred to in section 65 within the time
    referred to in that section;

  - [(c.1)]{.lawlabel} the applicant does not make a request for
    continued examination of an application for a patent and pay the
    prescribed fee in accordance with subsection 85.1(3);

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a notice of allowance is sent under subsection
    86(1), (6), (10) or (12) or 199(2) or (5) and the applicant does not
    pay the final fee set out in item 14 of Schedule 2 within the time
    referred to in the applicable subsection;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the applicant does not reply in good faith to a
    conditional notice of allowance of the Commissioner sent under
    subsection 86(1.1) and pay the final fee set out in item 14 of
    Schedule 2 within the time referred to in that subsection; or

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the applicant does not comply with a notice of the
    Commissioner referred to in subsection 155.5(6) within the time
    referred to in that subsection.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Paragraphs (1)(d) and (e) do not apply in respect of
  a notice of allowance or a conditional notice of allowance that is set
  aside in accordance with subsection 85.1(4).

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 42](#1353831-1375285){.wb-lbx

  role="button"}

  role="button"}
:::

Version](section-203-20221003.html "Link to previous version of section 203"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Refund of final fee

**[[204]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-204 .sectionLabel}** If a category 3
application is deemed to be abandoned under paragraph 73(1)(f) of the
Act, as it read immediately before October 30, 2019, and is subsequently
reinstated,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the Commissioner must refund the final fee paid in
  respect of that application if a request for a refund of the fee is
  received not later than one month after the date of the reinstatement
  of the application; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the final fee paid in respect of that application
  has not been refunded, despite subsections 86(1), (1.1), (6), (10) and
  (12), the Commissioner must not require payment of that fee in any
  notice of allowance or conditional notice of allowance sent after the
  reinstatement of the application.

::: HistoricalNote

### [DIVISION 5]{.HLabel2}[Rules Applicable to Certain Patents]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1183746 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of subsections 97(2) and
(3)

- **[[205]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-205
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subsections 97(2) and (3) do not
  apply in respect of a patent granted on the basis of a category 1
  application.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of subsections 187(2)
  and (3) of former Rules

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsections 187(2) and (3) of the former Rules
  continue to apply in respect of a patent issued on the basis of a
  category 1 application, except that the reference to subsection (1) in
  subsection 187(2) is to be read as a reference to subsection 97(1) of
  these Rules.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2022-120, s. 44\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 44](#1353838-1375292){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353838-1375292.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-205-20191030.html "Link to previous version of section 205"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of certain provisions of
former Rules to patents granted on basis of category 1 application

**[[206]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-206 .sectionLabel}** Subsections 3(9) and
182(1) to (3) of the former Rules and item 32 of Schedule II to those
Rules continue to apply in respect of the applicable fee payable to
maintain the rights accorded by a patent granted on the basis of a
category 1 application, if the time --- not including the period of
grace --- referred to in that item for the payment of that fee ends
before October 30, 2019.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 55](#1353871-1375437){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353871-1375437.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-206-20191030.html "Link to previous version of section 206"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of certain provisions of
former Rules to patents granted on basis of category 3 application

- **[[207]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-207
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subsection 3(8) and sections 100 and
  101 of the former Rules and item 31 of Schedule II to those Rules
  continue to apply in respect of the applicable fee payable to maintain
  the rights accorded by a patent granted on the basis of a category 3
  application, if the time --- not including the period of grace ---
  referred to in that item for the payment of that fee ends before
  October 30, 2019.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension of time

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Commissioner is authorized to extend the time for
  payment of the fee referred to in subsection 3(8) of the former Rules
  in respect of a patent issued on the basis of a category 3 application
  --- after that time, including a period of grace, has expired --- if
  the Commissioner considers that the circumstances justify the
  extension and if the conditions referred to in subsection 3(3) of
  these Rules are met.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 45](#1353842-1375296){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353842-1375296.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 55](#1353871-1375439){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353871-1375439.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-207-20191030.html "Link to previous version of section 207"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maintenance fee --- patent

**[[208]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-208 .sectionLabel}** In respect of a patent
granted on the basis of a category 1 application, the reference to "set
out in item 25 of Schedule 2" in subsection 112(1) is to be read as a
reference to "set out in item 1 of Schedule 3".

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Patent not invalid

- **[[209]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-209 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  patent that was granted on the basis of an international application
  must not be declared invalid by reason only that a fee referred to in
  section 58 of the former Rules was not paid.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in respect of a patent
  that was granted before October 30, 2019 or a reissued patent if the
  original patent was granted before that date.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 55](#1353871-1375443){.wb-lbx

  role="button"}

### [DIVISION 6]{.HLabel2}[Other Rules]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1183768 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- national phase entry date

- **[[210]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-210 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of these Rules, if the applicant of an international
  application for which the [[international filing
  date]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as defined in section 141, precedes
  October 30, 2019 has, before October 30, 2019, complied with the
  requirements of subsection 58(1) and, if applicable, subsection 58(2)
  of the former Rules, the national phase entry date in respect of that
  application is the date on which the applicant complied with those
  requirements, or, if the applicant complied with those requirements on
  different dates, the latest of those dates.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fee considered paid

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the Commissioner has, under section 190 of these
  Rules or subsection 26(3) of the former Rules, extended the time for
  the payment of a fee referred to in subsection 3(5) or (7) of the
  former Rules, and the fee is paid before the end of the extended
  period, for the purposes of subsection (1), that fee is considered to
  have been paid on the day on which the amount of the small entity fee
  was paid.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- national phase entry date

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In these Rules, if the applicant of an international
  application for which the [[international filing
  date]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as defined in section 141, precedes
  October 30, 2019 has, on or after October 30, 2019, complied with the
  requirements of subparagraphs 154(3)(a)(ii) to (iv) and paragraph
  154(3)(b), the national phase entry date in respect of that
  application is the date on which the applicant complied with those
  requirements, or, if the applicant complied with those requirements on
  different dates, the latest of those dates.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Small entity declaration

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of subsection (3), an applicant is
  not considered to have paid the fee referred to in clause
  154(3)(a)(iii)(A) or 154(3)(b)(i)(A) or (ii)(A) until the small entity
  declaration has been filed.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 46](#1353844-1375300){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353844-1375300.html"
  role="button"}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension of time --- section 208

**[[211]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-211 .sectionLabel}** The Commissioner is
authorized to extend the time for the payment of a fee referred to in
subsection 112(1), as modified by section 208, after the expiry of that
time, if the Commissioner considers that the circumstances justify the
extension and if the conditions referred to in subsection 3(3) are met.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 47](#1353846-1375304){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353846-1375304.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Previous
Version](section-211-20191030.html "Link to previous version of section 211"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension of period established by
Commissioner

**[[212]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-212 .sectionLabel}** If, under paragraph
73(1)(a) of the Act, as it read immediately before October 30, 2019, the
Commissioner established a shorter period for replying in good faith to
any requisition made by an examiner in connection with an examination,
the Commissioner is authorized to extend that period for up to six
months after the requisition was made --- whether that time has expired
or not --- if the Commissioner considers that the circumstances justify
the extension and if, before the expiration of that period, the
extension is applied for and the fee set out in item 1 of Schedule 2 is
paid.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 55](#1353871-1375451){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353871-1375451.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-212-20191030.html "Link to previous version of section 212"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension of time fixed by former Rules

**[[213]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-213 .sectionLabel}** If, before October 30,
2019, a requisition referred to in section 23, 25, 37 or 94 of the
former Rules was sent by notice by the Commissioner, the Commissioner is
authorized to extend the time period to reply in good faith under that
section to the requisition --- whether that period has expired or not
--- if the Commissioner considers that the circumstances justify the
extension and if, before the expiration of that period, the extension is
applied for and the fee referred to in item 1 of Schedule 2 to these
Rules is paid.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 55](#1353871-1375455){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353871-1375455.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Previous
Version](section-213-20191030.html "Link to previous version of section 213"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[214]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-214 .sectionLabel}** [\[Repealed,
SOR/2023-113, s. 10\]]{.Repealed}

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-214-20221003.html "Link to previous version of section 214"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Documents not in English or French

**[[215]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-215 .sectionLabel}** Despite section 15, the
Commissioner must have regard to any document or part of a document that
is submitted or made available in a language other than English or
French under subsection 196(1) of these Rules, or provided under
subsection 29(1) or paragraph 58(1)(a) of the former Rules or filed
under section 89 or 180 of the former Rules, in a language other than
English or French.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 48](#1353848-1375315){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353848-1375315.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Previous
Version](section-215-20191030.html "Link to previous version of section 215"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Patent agent deemed to be appointed

**[[216]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-216 .sectionLabel}** If, before October 30,
2019, a patent agent was appointed in the petition or in a notice to
that effect signed by the applicant and submitted to the Commissioner,
the appointment of the patent agent is deemed to have been made in
accordance with section 27.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 55](#1353871-1375459){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353871-1375459.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Previous
Version](section-216-20191030.html "Link to previous version of section 216"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Associate patent agent deemed to be
appointed

**[[217]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-217 .sectionLabel}** If, before October 30,
2019, an associate patent agent was appointed in the petition or in a
notice that was signed by the patent agent who appointed the associate
patent agent and submitted to the Commissioner, the appointment of the
associate patent agent is deemed to have been made in accordance with
section 28.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 55](#1353871-1375463){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353871-1375463.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Previous
Version](section-217-20191030.html "Link to previous version of section 217"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Representation --- application filed
before October 30, 2019

**[[218]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-218 .sectionLabel}** In respect of an
application for a patent for which the filing date is before October 30,
2019 --- other than a divisional application that has a presentation
date on or after October 30, 2019 --- for which there are joint
applicants and in respect of which no common representative is appointed
under paragraph 26(3)(a) or (c), in respect of which no correction or
decision referred to in subsection 26(6) was made --- other than a
decision made before October 30, 2019 --- and in respect of which
subsection 26(9) does not apply,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} if, before October 30, 2019, no patent agent residing
  in Canada is appointed

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} subsections 26(4) to (6) do not apply, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsection 26(11), the joint applicant
    who, before October 30, 2019 was the authorized correspondent under
    the former Rules, is deemed to be appointed as the common
    representative; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} if, before October 30, 2019, a patent agent residing
  in Canada is appointed,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if the appointment of that patent agent has been
    revoked, the joint applicant whose name appears first when listed in
    alphabetical order at the time of the revocation is, subject to
    subsection 26(11), deemed to be appointed as the common
    representative, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in any other case,

    - [(A)]{.lawlabel} subsections 26(4) to (6) and paragraph 27(7)(a)
      do not apply,

    - [(B)]{.lawlabel} any appointment of a patent agent by the
      applicants on or after October 30, 2019 may, despite subsection
      27(3), be made only by a notice to that effect signed by all of
      the applicants and submitted to the Commissioner, and

    - [(C)]{.lawlabel} an appointment of a patent agent may be revoked
      by submitting to the Commissioner a notice to that effect signed
      by all of the applicants or by that patent agent.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 49](#1353850-1375319){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353850-1375319.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 55](#1353871-1375468){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353871-1375468.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-218-20191030.html "Link to previous version of section 218"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Representation --- patent granted before
October 30, 2019

**[[219]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-219 .sectionLabel}** In respect of a patent
--- other than a reissued patent --- granted before October 30, 2019 for
which there are joint patentees and in respect of which no common
representative is appointed under paragraph 26(3)(a) and in respect of
which subsection 26(9) does not apply,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} if, immediately before the patent was granted, no
  patent agent residing in Canada was appointed in respect of the
  application on which the patent was based and if the joint applicant
  who, immediately before the patent was granted, was the authorized
  correspondent under the former Rules, is a patentee before October 30,
  2019,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} subsection 26(7) does not apply, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsection 26(11), that applicant is
    deemed to be appointed as the common representative; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} subsection 26(7) and paragraph 27(7)(a) do not
    apply,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} any appointment of a patent agent by the patentees
    on or after October 30, 2019 may, despite subsection 27(3), be made
    only by a notice to that effect signed by all of the patentees and
    submitted to the Commissioner, and

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} an appointment of a patent agent, including a
    deemed appointment, may be revoked by submitting to the Commissioner
    a notice to that effect signed by all of the patentees or by that
    patent agent.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 55](#1353871-1375472){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353871-1375472.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Previous
Version](section-219-20191030.html "Link to previous version of section 219"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Representation --- patent reissued before
October 30, 2019

**[[220]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-220 .sectionLabel}** In respect of a patent
that is reissued before October 30, 2019 for which there are joint
patentees and in respect of which no common representative is appointed
under paragraph 26(3)(a) and in respect of which subsection 26(9) does
not apply,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} if, immediately before the original patent was
  granted, no patent agent residing in Canada was appointed in respect
  of the application on which the original patent was based and if the
  joint applicant who, immediately before the original patent was
  granted, was the authorized correspondent under the former Rules, is a
  patentee of the reissued patent before October 30, 2019,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} subsection 26(8) does not apply, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsection 26(11), that applicant is
    deemed to be appointed as the common representative; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in any other case,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} subsection 26(8) and paragraph 27(7)(a) do not
    apply,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} any appointment of a patent agent by the patentees
    on or after October 30, 2019 may, despite subsection 27(3), be made
    only by a notice to that effect signed by all of the patentees and
    submitted to the Commissioner, and

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} an appointment of a patent agent, including a
    deemed appointment, may be revoked by submitting to the Commissioner
    a notice to that effect signed by all of the patentees or by that
    patent agent.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 55](#1353871-1375476){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353871-1375476.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-220-20191030.html "Link to previous version of section 220"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Representation --- patent granted on or
after October 30, 2019

**[[221]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-221 .sectionLabel}** In respect of a patent,
other than a reissued patent, granted on or after October 30, 2019, on
the basis of an application for a patent for which the filing date is
before October 30, 2019, for which there are joint patentees and in
respect of which no common representative is appointed under paragraph
26(3)(a), if, immediately before the patent was granted, no common
representative was appointed in respect of that application,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} subsection 26(7) and paragraph 27(7)(a) do not apply;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} any appointment of a patent agent on or after October
  30, 2019 may, despite subsection 27(3), be made by the patentees only
  by a notice to that effect signed by all of the patentees and
  submitted to the Commissioner; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} an appointment of a patent agent, including a deemed
  appointment, may be revoked by submitting to the Commissioner a notice
  to that effect signed by all of the patentees or by that patent agent.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 50](#1353852-1375321){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353852-1375321.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 55](#1353871-1375484){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353871-1375484.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-221-20191030.html "Link to previous version of section 221"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Representation --- patent reissued on or
after October 30, 2019

**[[222]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-222 .sectionLabel}** In respect of a patent
that is reissued --- on the basis of an application for a patent for
which the filing date is before October 30, 2019 --- on or after October
30, 2019 for which there are joint patentees and in respect of which no
common representative is appointed under paragraph 26(3)(a), if,
immediately before the patent was reissued, no common representative was
appointed in respect of the original patent,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} subsection 26(8) and paragraph 27(7)(a) do not apply;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} any appointment of a patent agent on or after October
  30, 2019 may, despite subsection 27(3), be made by the patentees only
  by a notice to that effect signed by all of the patentees and
  submitted to the Commissioner; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} an appointment of a patent agent may be revoked by
  submitting to the Commissioner a notice to that effect signed by all
  of the patentees or by that patent agent.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 51](#1353854-1375327){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353854-1375327.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 55](#1353871-1375486){.wb-lbx

  role="button"}
:::

[Previous
Version](section-222-20191030.html "Link to previous version of section 222"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of section 37

**[[223]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-223 .sectionLabel}** Section 37 does not
apply in respect of business before the Patent Office for the purpose of
a procedure commenced before October 30, 2019.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 55](#1353871-1375490){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353871-1375490.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-223-20191030.html "Link to previous version of section 223"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Small entity declaration for patent or
application

- **[[224]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-224 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  small entity declaration filed before October 30, 2019 in respect of a
  patent or an application for a patent in accordance with section 3.01
  of the former Rules is considered to be filed in accordance with
  subsection 44(3) or 112(3) of these Rules, as applicable.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Small entity declaration --- patent

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A small entity declaration filed in respect of a
  patent in accordance with section 3.02 of the former Rules before
  October 30, 2019 is considered to be filed in accordance with
  subsection 122(4) of these Rules.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 55](#1353871-1375497){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353871-1375497.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-224-20191030.html "Link to previous version of section 224"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception to section 54 --- filing date
before June 2, 2007

- **[[225]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-225 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  respect of an application for a patent, other than a PCT national
  phase application, that has a filing date before June 2, 2007, the
  applicant, instead of complying with the requirements of section 54,
  may comply with

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the requirements of section 37 of the former Rules;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the requirements of section 77 of the [Patent
    Rules](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-423), as they read immediately before
    October 1, 2010; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the requirements of sections 37 and 77 of the
    [Patent Rules](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-423), as they read
    immediately before June 2, 2007.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- compliance with other
  requirements

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In respect of a PCT national phase application for
  which the filing date is before June 2, 2007, the applicant may,
  instead of complying with the requirements of section 54, comply with
  the requirements of paragraph (1)(a), (b) or (c) or file a declaration
  as to the applicant's entitlement on the filing date to apply for and
  be granted a patent in accordance with Rule 4.17 of the Regulations
  under the PCT.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception to section 54 --- filing date
before October 1, 2010

- **[[226]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-226 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  respect of an application for a patent, other than a PCT national
  phase application, for which the filing date is June 2, 2007 or later
  but before October 1, 2010, the applicant, instead of complying with
  the requirements of section 54, may comply with

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the requirements of section 37 of the former Rules;
    or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the requirements of section 77 of the [Patent
    Rules](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-423), as they read immediately before
    October 1, 2010.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- compliance with other
  requirements

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In respect of a PCT national phase application for
  which the filing date is June 2, 2007 or later but before October 1,
  2010, the applicant may, instead of complying with the requirements of
  section 54, comply with the requirements of paragraph (1)(a) or (b) or
  file a declaration as to the applicant's entitlement on the filing
  date to apply for and be granted a patent in accordance with Rule 4.17
  of the Regulations under the PCT.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception to section 54 --- filing date
before October 30, 2019

**[[227]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-227 .sectionLabel}** In respect of an
application for a patent for which the filing date is October 1, 2010 or
later but before October 30, 2019, the applicant, instead of complying
with the requirements of section 54, may comply with the requirements of
section 37 of the former Rules.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 55](#1353871-1377335){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353871-1377335.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-227-20191030.html "Link to previous version of section 227"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Clarification

**[[228]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-228 .sectionLabel}** For greater certainty,
the dates prescribed by sections 69 and 113 do not include any dates
that precede October 30, 2019.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 55](#1353871-1375505){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353871-1375505.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-228-20191030.html "Link to previous version of section 228"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Final fee paid before October 30, 2019

**[[229]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-229 .sectionLabel}** If, before October 30,
2019, the applicant of an application for a patent for which the filing
date is before that date, paid, in respect of that application, the
applicable final fee set out in item 6 of Schedule II to the former
Rules and that fee was not refunded before that date,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the reference to "on or before the day on which the
  final fee set out in item 14 of Schedule 2 is paid or, if the final
  fee is refunded, on or before the day on which the final fee is paid
  again" in subsection 73(5), paragraph 100(2)(a), section 105 and
  paragraph 106(a) is to be read, in respect of that application, as a
  reference to "on or before the day on which the applicable final fee
  set out in item 6 of Schedule II to the former Rules is paid or, if
  the final fee is refunded, on or before the day on which the final fee
  set out in item 14 of Schedule 2 is paid"; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the reference to "after the day on which the final
  fee set out in item 14 of Schedule 2 is paid or, if the final fee is
  refunded, after the day on which the final fee is paid again" in
  section 127 is to be read as "after the day on which the applicable
  final fee set out in item 6 of Schedule II to the former Rules is paid
  or, if the final fee is refunded, after the day on which the final fee
  set out in item 14 of Schedule 2 is paid".

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 52](#1353856-1375332){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353856-1375332.html"
  role="button"}
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 53](#1353858-1375362){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353858-1375362.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Previous
Version](section-229-20191030.html "Link to previous version of section 229"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of allowance considered not sent

**[[230]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-230 .sectionLabel}** For greater certainty,
a notice of allowance is considered not to have been sent if it was
deemed not to have been sent before October 30, 2019.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 55](#1353871-1375509){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353871-1375509.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-230-20191030.html "Link to previous version of section 230"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of section 89

**[[231]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-231 .sectionLabel}** Section 89 does not
apply in respect of an application for a patent for which the
presentation date preceded October 30, 2019.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 55](#1353871-1375513){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353871-1375513.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-231-20191030.html "Link to previous version of section 231"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Periods prescribed by subsection 128(1)

- **[[232]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-232 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  greater certainty, the periods prescribed by paragraph 128(1)(a), (b)
  or (d) do not include any period that begins less than six months
  after October 30, 2019.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Periods prescribed by subsection 128(1)

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A period prescribed by paragraph 128(1)(a) or (b) or
  paragraph 128(1)(d), as it read immediately before the day on which
  this subsection comes into force, that began before that day but did
  not end on or before that day is deemed to have ended on that day if,
  on or before that day,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the small entity fee was paid even though the fee
    referred to in paragraph 128(1)(a) or that paragraph 128(1)(d)
    should have been paid, and a small entity declaration was filed in
    accordance with subsection 112(3) in respect of the patent or in
    accordance with subsection 44(3) in respect of the application on
    the basis of which the patent was granted;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an insufficient amount was paid for the fee
    referred to in subsection 80(1) for the examination of the
    application of the patent and the Commissioner sent a notice to the
    applicant acknowledging that the request for examination was made in
    accordance with subsection 35(2) of the Act; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the Commissioner provided erroneous information in
    writing concerning the amount of a fee referred to in paragraph
    128(1)(a) or that paragraph 128(1)(d) and the applicant or patentee
    paid an insufficient amount that was equal to the amount specified
    in the erroneous information.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 55](#1353871-1375517){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353871-1375517.html"
  role="button"}

  role="button"}

:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Publication in Canadian Patent Office
Record

**[[233]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-233 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
paragraph 130(1)(c), an application that was advertised in the Canadian
Patent Office Record before October 30, 2019 is considered to have been
advertised on the website of the Canadian Intellectual Property Office
on the day on which it was advertised in the Canadian Patent Office
Record.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 55](#1353871-1375521){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353871-1375521.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Previous
Version](section-233-20191030.html "Link to previous version of section 233"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of subparagraph
154(3)(a)(i)

**[[234]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-234 .sectionLabel}** Subparagraph
154(3)(a)(i) does not apply in respect of an international application
for which the [[international filing date]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as
defined in section 141, precedes October 30, 2019.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 55](#1353871-1375525){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353871-1375525.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Previous
Version](section-234-20191030.html "Link to previous version of section 234"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception to section 162

**[[235]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-235 .sectionLabel}** If the right of
priority in respect of a previously regularly filed application, on
which the request for priority in relation to a pending application is
based, was restored under Rule 26*bis*.3 of the Regulations under the
PCT before October 30, 2019, section 162 does not apply in respect of
that previously regularly filed application.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-120, s. 55](#1353871-1375529){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1353871-1375529.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-235-20191030.html "Link to previous version of section 235"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Filing date before December 1, 2020

**[[236]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-236 .sectionLabel}** The fees set out in
paragraph 8(e) of Schedule 2 do not apply in respect of an application
for a patent for which the filing date is before December 1, 2020.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2024-241, s. 20](#1496948-1505927){.wb-lbx

  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of paragraph 84(2)(c)

**[[237]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-237 .sectionLabel}** Paragraph 84(2)(c) does
not apply in respect of a request for continued examination made before
the day on which this section comes into force.

  line](/img/ii_spacer.gif){.Leader-solid}, granted on ![blank

  entitled![blank line](/img/ii_spacer.gif){.Leader-solid}, ![blank

  inadvertence, and without any wilful intent to defraud or mislead the
  public,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} made the specification too broad, claiming more
    than that of which the patentee or the person through whom the
    patentee claims was the inventor; or

::: ScheduleRP
## RELATED PROVISIONS {#related-provisions .scheduleTitleText}

- --- SOR/2021-131, s. 27

  - - [27]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} The following definitions apply
      in this section.

      [[former Rules]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

      :   [[former Rules]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the [Patent
          Rules](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-423) as they read immediately
          before the day on which these Rules come into
          force. ([anciennes règles]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

      [[new Rules]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

      :   [[new Rules]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the [Patent
          Rules](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-423) as they read on the day on
          which these Rules come into force. ([nouvelles
          règles]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

    - [(2)]{.lawlabel} On the day on which these Rules come into force,

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the appointment of any patent agent who is not
        resident in Canada is revoked;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an associate patent agent who was appointed by
        a patent agent who is not resident in Canada is deemed to have
        been appointed as a patent agent under section 27 of the former
        Rules;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} all the patent agents who are members of a firm
        that was appointed as a patent agent under section 27 of the
        former Rules are deemed to be appointed under section 27 of the
        new Rules; and

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} all the patent agents who are members of a firm
        that was appointed as an associate patent agent under section 28
        of the former Rules are deemed to be appointed under section 28
        of the new Rules.

- --- SOR/2022-120, s. 60

  - [60]{.lawlabel} In respect of an application for a patent that has a
    filing date before the day on which this section comes into force, a
    sequence listing may, for the purposes of subsection 58(1) of the
    [Patent Rules](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-423), comply with the [[PCT
    sequence listing standard]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as defined in
    subsection 1(1) of the [Patent Rules](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-423)
    as they read immediately before the day on which this section comes
    into force.

- --- SOR/2022-120, s. 61

  - [61]{.lawlabel} Subsections 80(1) and 85.1(1) and subparagraphs
    87(1)(a)(iii) and (b)(iii) of the [Patent
    Rules](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-423) do not apply in respect of the
    examination of an application for a patent if all of the following
    actions are taken before October 3, 2022:

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the request for the examination of the
      application is made;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the prescribed fee for the examination of an
      application referred to in subsection 80(1) of the [Patent
      Rules](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-423), as it read immediately before
      October 3, 2022, is paid; and

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, the prescribed late fee referred
      to in section 82 of the [Patent
      Rules](/eng/regulations/SOR-96-423) is paid.
:::
